## 3.5 Checkout Module

The Checkout Module enables customers to review their order, select a delivery address, choose a payment method, verify order details, and confirm the purchase before the order is placed.

The functional requirements included in the Checkout Module are:

### 3.5.1 Proceed to Checkout

#### 3.5.1.1 Description

The Proceed to Checkout functionality enables an authenticated customer to initiate the checkout process after reviewing the shopping cart. During this process, the system validates the shopping cart, verifies restaurant availability, confirms inventory availability, refreshes pricing information, validates promotional offers, verifies delivery serviceability, recalculates all applicable charges, and creates a secure checkout session before allowing the customer to continue.

The checkout process acts as the transition between the Shopping Cart Module and the Order Placement process. It ensures that all information displayed to the customer reflects the latest business rules, pricing policies, inventory status, restaurant availability, delivery constraints, and promotional eligibility. The system shall prevent customers from proceeding whenever mandatory validations fail, thereby avoiding invalid orders, payment failures, pricing inconsistencies, or delivery issues.

Only validated shopping carts shall be allowed to enter the checkout workflow. All checkout activities shall be securely associated with the authenticated customer and shall be tracked using a unique checkout session identifier.

#### 3.5.1.2 Actors

##### Primary Actor

- Customer

##### Supporting Actors

- Authentication Service
- Shopping Cart Service
- Checkout Service
- Restaurant Service
- Menu Service
- Inventory Service
- Pricing Service
- Promotion Service
- Delivery Service
- Tax Calculation Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service

#### 3.5.1.3 Preconditions

The following conditions shall be satisfied before a customer can proceed to checkout:

1. Customer shall be successfully authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Customer account shall not be suspended or blocked.
4. Shopping cart shall exist.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one active item.
6. All shopping cart items shall belong to the same restaurant.
7. Restaurant shall be active.
8. Restaurant shall be accepting new orders.
9. Restaurant shall not be temporarily closed.
10. Restaurant shall not be permanently closed.
11. Requested item quantities shall be available.
12. Every menu item shall be active.
13. Customer shall have selected a valid delivery location or be eligible to select one during checkout.
14. Required backend services shall be available.
15. System shall be operational.

#### 3.5.1.4 Trigger

The checkout process is initiated when the customer clicks the **Proceed to Checkout** button from the Shopping Cart page after reviewing the selected products.

#### 3.5.1.5 Input Fields

| Field | Description | Mandatory |
|--------|-------------|-----------|
| Customer ID | Unique identifier of the authenticated customer | Yes |
| Cart ID | Unique shopping cart identifier | Yes |
| Restaurant ID | Restaurant associated with the shopping cart | Yes |
| Delivery Address ID | Selected delivery address | Yes |
| Customer Latitude | Customer delivery latitude | Yes |
| Customer Longitude | Customer delivery longitude | Yes |
| Device Type | Android, iOS or Web | Yes |
| Application Version | Client application version | Yes |
| Session ID | User session identifier | Yes |
| Currency | Currency used for order calculation | Yes |
| Language Preference | Preferred application language | No |
| Coupon Code | Promotional coupon selected by customer | No |
| Wallet Preference | Indicates whether wallet balance should be considered | No |
| Delivery Preference | Immediate or Scheduled delivery | No |
| Requested Delivery Time | Preferred delivery time for scheduled orders | No |

#### 3.5.1.6 Business Rules

1. Customers shall not proceed to checkout with an empty shopping cart.
2. Customers shall be authenticated before accessing the Checkout page.
3. Customer accounts shall be active and not suspended, blocked, or permanently deactivated.
4. Every shopping cart shall belong to only one authenticated customer.
5. All products in the shopping cart shall belong to a single restaurant.
6. The selected restaurant shall have the status **OPEN**.
7. Restaurants that are temporarily unavailable shall not accept checkout requests.
8. Restaurants that have stopped accepting orders shall not allow checkout.
9. Every menu item shall be active and available for ordering.
10. Requested quantities shall not exceed the available inventory.
11. Product prices shall always be refreshed before checkout begins.
12. Taxes shall be recalculated during every checkout request.
13. Delivery charges shall be recalculated using the selected delivery address.
14. Platform fees shall be recalculated using the latest pricing policy.
15. Packing charges shall be recalculated whenever applicable.
16. Promotional offers shall be revalidated before checkout.
17. Expired promotional offers shall be removed automatically.
18. Invalid coupon codes shall not be carried forward.
19. Wallet balance shall not be reserved until payment is successfully completed.
20. The restaurant's minimum order value shall be satisfied before checkout.
21. Maximum order value restrictions shall be validated whenever configured.
22. Delivery shall only be allowed within the restaurant's configured delivery radius.
23. Delivery availability shall be verified using the selected delivery address.
24. Scheduled delivery shall be available only if supported by the restaurant.
25. Checkout shall not create an order record until the customer explicitly confirms the order.
26. Only one active checkout session shall exist for a shopping cart.
27. Every checkout session shall have a unique Checkout Session ID.
28. Checkout sessions shall expire automatically after the configured inactivity timeout.
29. Expired checkout sessions shall require the customer to restart checkout.
30. Shopping cart data shall be locked during payment initiation to prevent concurrent modifications.
31. Any pricing changes occurring before order confirmation shall be reflected immediately.
32. System-generated timestamps shall use the server's standard time.
33. Every checkout initiation shall be recorded in the audit log.
34. Sensitive customer information shall be transmitted only through secure communication channels.
35. The system shall maintain complete traceability of every checkout request for auditing purposes.

#### 3.5.1.7 Validations

The system shall perform the following validations before allowing the customer to proceed to checkout:

1. Verify customer authentication status.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify customer session validity.
4. Verify shopping cart ownership.
5. Verify shopping cart exists.
6. Verify shopping cart is not empty.
7. Verify all cart items are active.
8. Verify requested quantities are greater than zero.
9. Verify sufficient inventory exists for every cart item.
10. Verify restaurant exists.
11. Verify restaurant status is **OPEN**.
12. Verify restaurant is accepting new orders.
13. Verify restaurant has not exceeded its order capacity.
14. Verify selected menu items are available.
15. Verify product prices have not changed.
16. Verify applied coupons are still valid.
17. Verify promotional eligibility.
18. Verify minimum order value.
19. Verify maximum order value restrictions.
20. Verify delivery address exists.
21. Verify delivery address belongs to the authenticated customer.
22. Verify delivery address falls within the restaurant's delivery zone.
23. Verify delivery service availability.
24. Verify scheduled delivery availability, if selected.
25. Verify tax calculation service response.
26. Verify pricing calculation service response.
27. Verify checkout session creation.
28. Verify mandatory checkout information is available before redirecting the customer.

#### 3.5.1.8 Main Flow

1. Customer reviews the shopping cart and confirms the selected products.
2. Customer clicks the **Proceed to Checkout** button.
3. System receives the checkout initiation request.
4. System authenticates the customer session.
5. System validates the customer's account status.
6. System retrieves the latest shopping cart information.
7. System verifies that the shopping cart belongs to the authenticated customer.
8. System validates that the shopping cart is not empty.
9. System verifies that all products belong to the same restaurant.
10. System retrieves the latest restaurant information.
11. System verifies that the restaurant is currently operational.
12. System verifies that the restaurant is accepting new orders.
13. System validates the availability of every product in the shopping cart.
14. System validates the requested quantity of each product.
15. System retrieves the latest product prices.
16. System recalculates the subtotal using the latest pricing.
17. System validates all applied promotional offers.
18. System removes expired or invalid promotional offers automatically.
19. System recalculates applicable discounts.
20. System calculates taxes based on the delivery location.
21. System calculates delivery charges.
22. System calculates platform fees and packing charges.
23. System calculates the latest final payable amount.
24. System validates the selected delivery location.
25. System verifies delivery service availability for the selected address.
26. System generates a unique Checkout Session ID.
27. System stores the validated checkout session.
28. System records the checkout initiation in the audit log.
29. System redirects the customer to the Checkout page.
30. System displays the latest order summary, delivery details, pricing information, and available checkout options.

#### 3.5.1.9 Alternate Flows

##### AF-1: Shopping Cart Empty

1. System detects that the shopping cart contains no items.
2. System displays an appropriate error message.
3. Customer remains on the Shopping Cart page.

##### AF-2: Customer Session Expired

1. System detects that the login session has expired.
2. Customer is redirected to the Login page.
3. Customer logs in again.
4. Checkout process restarts.

##### AF-3: Restaurant Closed

1. System determines that the restaurant is currently closed.
2. Checkout request is rejected.
3. Customer is informed that the restaurant is unavailable.

##### AF-4: Restaurant Not Accepting Orders

1. System identifies that the restaurant has paused order acceptance.
2. Checkout process is cancelled.
3. Customer receives an appropriate notification.

##### AF-5: Product Out of Stock

1. One or more products become unavailable.
2. System highlights unavailable products.
3. Customer updates the shopping cart.
4. Checkout validation restarts.

##### AF-6: Product Price Changed

1. System detects updated product pricing.
2. Shopping cart prices are refreshed.
3. Updated payable amount is displayed.
4. Customer reviews the revised order before continuing.

##### AF-7: Coupon Expired

1. Applied coupon is found to be expired or invalid.
2. System removes the coupon automatically.
3. Updated payable amount is displayed.

##### AF-8: Delivery Address Not Serviceable

1. Selected delivery address is outside the delivery zone.
2. Checkout process is paused.
3. Customer selects another delivery address.

##### AF-9: Checkout Session Expired

1. Checkout session exceeds the configured timeout.
2. Session is invalidated.
3. Customer starts a new checkout session.

##### AF-10: Internal System Failure

1. An unexpected system error occurs during checkout validation.
2. System records the error in the application logs.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout process is terminated safely.
5. Customer may retry later.

#### 3.5.1.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Proceed to Checkout process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. A unique checkout session shall be created for the customer.
2. The validated shopping cart shall be associated with the checkout session.
3. The latest pricing information shall be stored within the checkout session.
4. Applicable taxes, delivery charges, platform fees, packing charges, and discounts shall be finalized for the current session.
5. The selected restaurant information shall be validated and retained.
6. Product availability shall be confirmed at the time of checkout initiation.
7. Customer shall be redirected to the Checkout page.
8. Order summary shall be generated using the latest validated information.
9. Checkout activity shall be recorded in the audit logs.
10. The system shall be ready to accept delivery, payment, and order confirmation details.

#### 3.5.1.11 Success Response

Upon successful initiation of checkout, the system shall:

1. Display the Checkout page successfully.
2. Display the latest order summary.
3. Display selected restaurant information.
4. Display itemized pricing details.
5. Display applicable taxes and additional charges.
6. Display available delivery options.
7. Display available payment methods.
8. Generate and display the Checkout Session ID internally.
9. Allow the customer to continue with checkout activities.
10. Maintain the checkout session until completion or expiration.

#### 3.5.1.12 Failure Response

If checkout initiation fails, the system shall:

1. Prevent the customer from entering the Checkout page.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Highlight invalid shopping cart items whenever applicable.
4. Display unavailable products separately.
5. Display updated pricing if prices have changed.
6. Remove invalid promotional offers automatically.
7. Prevent creation of an invalid checkout session.
8. Record failure details in the application logs.
9. Maintain shopping cart integrity without data loss.
10. Allow the customer to retry after resolving the identified issues.

#### 3.5.1.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Proceed to Checkout functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can initiate checkout.
2. Checkout cannot be initiated with an empty shopping cart.
3. Shopping cart ownership is successfully validated.
4. All cart items belong to a single restaurant.
5. Restaurant availability is validated before checkout.
6. Product inventory is successfully validated.
7. Latest product pricing is always used.
8. Expired offers are automatically removed.
9. Delivery availability is successfully verified.
10. Applicable taxes and charges are accurately calculated.
11. Final payable amount is correctly displayed.
12. Checkout Session ID is successfully generated.
13. Customer is redirected to the Checkout page only after successful validation.
14. Invalid checkout requests are rejected with meaningful error messages.
15. Checkout initiation is recorded in the audit logs.
16. No order is created during the Proceed to Checkout process.
17. Customer data remains secure throughout the checkout initiation process.
18. The system maintains data consistency throughout the checkout workflow.
19. Checkout session remains available until confirmation or configured timeout.
20. The system complies with all configured business rules before allowing checkout.

#### 3.5.1.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to access the Checkout page.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before retrieving checkout information.
3. Customer data shall be transmitted only over secure HTTPS connections.
4. Checkout Session IDs shall be unique and difficult to predict.
5. Sensitive customer information shall not be exposed in URLs or application logs.
6. Checkout requests shall be protected against session hijacking and replay attacks.
7. Input data shall be validated to prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), and other injection attacks.
8. Customer shall only access their own shopping cart and checkout session.
9. Checkout sessions shall automatically expire after the configured inactivity timeout.
10. All sensitive operations shall comply with the application's security policies.

#### 3.5.1.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit events during the Proceed to Checkout process:

1. Checkout initiation timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID.
4. Shopping Cart ID.
5. Restaurant ID.
6. Customer IP Address.
7. Device Type.
8. Application Version.
9. Checkout initiation status (Success/Failure).
10. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
11. System-generated error code, if any.
12. Audit log timestamp.
13. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's retention policy.

#### 3.5.1.16 Assumptions

The Proceed to Checkout functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. The customer has successfully authenticated before initiating checkout.
2. The shopping cart contains valid products added through the application.
3. Restaurant operating hours and availability are maintained accurately by the Restaurant Management module.
4. Product inventory information is synchronized with the Inventory Management system.
5. Product pricing, taxes, platform fees, and delivery charges are configured correctly in the Pricing Service.
6. Delivery zones and delivery partner availability are maintained by the Delivery Management system.
7. All supporting backend services are operational and accessible.
8. Customer delivery addresses have been previously saved or will be provided during checkout.
9. Network connectivity between client and server is available throughout the checkout process.
10. The system clock is synchronized across all backend services.

#### 3.5.1.17 Dependencies

The Proceed to Checkout functionality depends on the successful availability of the following system components and services:

1. Authentication Service
2. Customer Management Module
3. Shopping Cart Module
4. Restaurant Management Module
5. Menu Management Module
6. Inventory Management Service
7. Pricing Service
8. Promotion and Coupon Service
9. Tax Calculation Service
10. Delivery Management Service
11. Address Management Module
12. Checkout Service
13. Session Management Service
14. Audit Logging Service
15. Notification Service
16. Database Management System
17. Application Configuration Service

### 3.5.2 Review Order Summary

#### 3.5.2.1 Description

The Review Order Summary use case enables the customer to review all order-related information before placing the order. The system shall present a comprehensive summary of the selected products, quantities, pricing, discounts, taxes, delivery charges, platform fees, delivery address, estimated delivery time, restaurant information, and the final payable amount.

This functionality ensures that customers have an opportunity to verify the accuracy and completeness of their order before proceeding to payment. Customers may modify eligible order details such as item quantity, delivery address, applied coupons, or special instructions before confirming the order.

The system shall retrieve the latest validated information from all dependent services to ensure that the displayed order summary reflects the most current state of the order.

---

#### 3.5.2.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Checkout Service
- Shopping Cart Service
- Restaurant Service
- Menu Management Service
- Pricing Service
- Inventory Service
- Tax Calculation Service
- Promotion and Coupon Service
- Delivery Service
- Address Management Service
- Authentication Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.2.3 Preconditions

Before reviewing the order summary, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall be valid and not expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. Shopping cart shall belong to the authenticated customer.
7. Restaurant shall be operational.
8. Restaurant shall be accepting orders.
9. All selected products shall remain available.
10. Product inventory shall be sufficient.
11. Delivery location shall be serviceable.
12. Delivery address shall be available.
13. Pricing service shall be accessible.
14. Tax calculation service shall be available.
15. Promotion service shall be available.
16. Delivery service shall be available.
17. Network connectivity shall be available.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.2.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer selects the **"Review Order Summary"** or **"Continue to Order Summary"** option from the Checkout page before proceeding to payment.

---

#### 3.5.2.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Unique checkout session identifier | Yes | Valid active session |
| Customer ID | Logged-in customer identifier | Yes | Must match authenticated customer |
| Shopping Cart ID | Customer shopping cart identifier | Yes | Must exist |
| Delivery Address ID | Selected delivery address | Yes | Must be serviceable |
| Coupon Code | Applied promotional coupon | No | Must be valid and active |
| Delivery Instructions | Additional delivery notes | No | Maximum configured length |
| Gift Option | Indicates gift delivery | No | Boolean value |
| Scheduled Delivery Time | Preferred delivery slot | No | Future valid time |
| Tip Amount | Optional delivery partner tip | No | Non-negative amount |
| Payment Preference | Selected payment method | No | Supported payment method |

#### 3.5.2.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to review the order summary.
2. The order summary shall be generated only for a valid checkout session.
3. The system shall retrieve the latest shopping cart information before displaying the order summary.
4. All product details shall reflect the most recent information available in the Menu Management system.
5. Product prices shall be recalculated before displaying the order summary.
6. Product availability shall be verified before generating the summary.
7. Out-of-stock products shall not be eligible for order confirmation.
8. Updated product prices shall override previously cached prices.
9. The system shall calculate taxes using the configured tax rules.
10. Platform fees shall be calculated according to the current pricing policy.
11. Delivery charges shall be calculated based on the customer's delivery location.
12. Packing charges shall be calculated according to restaurant configuration.
13. The final payable amount shall include all applicable charges and deductions.
14. Valid promotional offers shall be applied automatically where applicable.
15. Invalid or expired coupons shall be removed before displaying the summary.
16. Only one applicable coupon shall be applied if multiple coupons are not supported.
17. Restaurant-specific offers shall follow restaurant eligibility rules.
18. Delivery eligibility shall be verified before displaying the estimated delivery time.
19. Estimated delivery time shall be calculated using the latest delivery partner availability.
20. The selected delivery address shall be validated before displaying the summary.
21. Customers shall be allowed to modify eligible order details before proceeding to payment.
22. Any modification to cart items shall trigger recalculation of the complete order summary.
23. Changes to the delivery address shall recalculate delivery charges and estimated delivery time.
24. Removing a coupon shall immediately update the final payable amount.
25. The order summary shall always display the latest calculated values.
26. Currency shall remain consistent throughout the checkout process.
27. All monetary values shall be rounded according to the configured financial rounding policy.
28. The system shall display an itemized pricing breakdown.
29. Special delivery instructions shall be retained throughout the checkout session.
30. Reviewing the order summary shall not create an order.
31. No payment transaction shall be initiated during this use case.
32. Order data shall remain editable until the customer proceeds to payment.
33. The system shall record all significant checkout modifications in audit logs.
34. The order summary shall expire when the checkout session expires.
35. The system shall maintain complete data consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.2.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify shopping cart existence.
6. Verify shopping cart ownership.
7. Verify shopping cart is not empty.
8. Verify restaurant availability.
9. Verify restaurant is accepting orders.
10. Verify every selected product exists.
11. Verify every product is active.
12. Verify product inventory availability.
13. Verify product quantity limits.
14. Verify latest product pricing.
15. Verify delivery address availability.
16. Verify delivery address belongs to the customer.
17. Verify delivery area is serviceable.
18. Verify coupon validity.
19. Verify coupon expiration date.
20. Verify coupon eligibility conditions.
21. Verify applicable promotional offers.
22. Verify tax calculation completion.
23. Verify delivery charge calculation.
24. Verify platform fee calculation.
25. Verify payable amount calculation accuracy.
26. Verify checkout session timeout.
27. Verify dependent services are available.
28. Verify mandatory checkout information is present before displaying the order summary.

#### 3.5.2.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Review Order Summary process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The latest validated order summary shall be generated and displayed to the customer.
2. All product information shall reflect the latest data available from the Menu Management system.
3. Product availability shall be verified for all selected items.
4. Latest product pricing shall be applied to the order summary.
5. Applicable discounts shall be calculated and displayed.
6. Taxes, delivery charges, platform fees, and packing charges shall be finalized for the current checkout session.
7. The final payable amount shall be accurately calculated and presented.
8. Delivery address and estimated delivery time shall be validated.
9. The checkout session shall remain active until completion or timeout.
10. The customer shall be eligible to proceed to the Payment module.

---

#### 3.5.2.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Review Order Summary process, the system shall:

1. Display a complete itemized order summary.
2. Display restaurant information.
3. Display ordered products and quantities.
4. Display individual product prices.
5. Display applied discounts and promotional offers.
6. Display calculated taxes.
7. Display delivery charges.
8. Display platform fees and packing charges.
9. Display the final payable amount.
10. Display delivery address and estimated delivery time.
11. Display delivery instructions, if provided.
12. Allow the customer to proceed to the Payment module.
13. Preserve the checkout session for subsequent checkout activities.

---

#### 3.5.2.12 Failure Response

If the Review Order Summary process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Prevent the customer from proceeding to payment.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Identify unavailable or invalid products.
4. Highlight pricing changes whenever applicable.
5. Remove invalid promotional offers automatically.
6. Inform the customer about delivery-related issues.
7. Prevent display of inaccurate payable amounts.
8. Preserve customer shopping cart information.
9. Record failure details in application logs.
10. Allow the customer to retry after resolving identified issues.

---

#### 3.5.2.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Review Order Summary functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can review the order summary.
2. The checkout session is successfully validated.
3. Shopping cart ownership is verified.
4. Latest product information is displayed.
5. Product inventory is successfully validated.
6. Latest product pricing is always used.
7. Applicable discounts are accurately calculated.
8. Taxes are calculated correctly.
9. Delivery charges are accurately calculated.
10. Platform fees and packing charges are displayed correctly.
11. Final payable amount is calculated accurately.
12. Invalid products cannot proceed to payment.
13. Expired coupons are removed automatically.
14. Updated delivery estimates are displayed.
15. Customer can modify eligible order details.
16. Order summary is recalculated after every modification.
17. Customer cannot proceed with invalid checkout information.
18. Checkout session integrity is maintained.
19. Audit logs are generated successfully.
20. Customer can proceed to payment only after successful validation.

---

#### 3.5.2.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall access the Review Order Summary page.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before displaying order details.
3. All communications shall occur over secure HTTPS connections.
4. Checkout Session IDs shall be securely generated and validated.
5. Customers shall only access their own checkout sessions.
6. Sensitive customer information shall not be exposed in URLs or application logs.
7. Input data shall be validated to prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), and other injection attacks.
8. Checkout sessions shall automatically expire after the configured inactivity timeout.
9. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational security policies.
10. All security-related events shall be recorded for auditing purposes.

#### 3.5.2.16 Assumptions

The Review Order Summary functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

2. A valid checkout session exists.
3. Shopping cart information is synchronized with backend services.

5. Product inventory information is current.

### 3.5.3 Select Delivery Address

#### 3.5.3.1 Description

The Select Delivery Address use case enables the customer to choose the delivery location for the current order during the checkout process. The system shall retrieve all active delivery addresses associated with the authenticated customer's account and allow the customer to select an existing address or add a new address.

The selected delivery address shall be validated to ensure it falls within the restaurant's serviceable delivery area. Upon successful validation, the system shall calculate the applicable delivery charges, estimate the delivery time, and update the checkout session with the selected delivery address.

If the selected address is not serviceable, the system shall notify the customer and prevent progression to the next checkout stage until a valid address is selected.

---

#### 3.5.3.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Customer Management Service
- Address Management Service
- Checkout Service
- Restaurant Service
- Delivery Service
- Geo Location Service
- Pricing Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.3.3 Preconditions

Before selecting a delivery address, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. Restaurant shall be operational.
7. Restaurant shall be accepting orders.
8. Customer shall have permission to access the checkout session.
9. Address Management Service shall be available.
10. Delivery Service shall be operational.
11. Geo Location Service shall be available.
12. Pricing Service shall be available.
13. Customer profile shall exist.
14. Customer shall have at least one saved address or be allowed to add a new address.
15. Restaurant delivery configuration shall be available.
16. Network connectivity shall be available.
17. All dependent backend services shall be operational.
18. System configuration for delivery zones shall be available.

---

#### 3.5.3.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer selects the **"Select Delivery Address"**, **"Change Address"**, or **"Add New Address"** option during the checkout process.

---

#### 3.5.3.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Address ID | Selected delivery address | Yes | Must exist and belong to customer |
| Recipient Name | Name of the recipient | Yes | Valid characters only |
| Mobile Number | Recipient contact number | Yes | Valid mobile number |
| House/Flat Number | House or apartment number | Yes | Cannot be empty |
| Street Address | Street and locality details | Yes | Maximum configured length |
| Landmark | Nearby landmark | No | Maximum configured length |
| City | Delivery city | Yes | Must be supported |
| State | Delivery state | Yes | Must be supported |
| Postal Code | Delivery postal/ZIP code | Yes | Valid serviceable postal code |
| Latitude | GPS latitude | No | Valid coordinate |
| Longitude | GPS longitude | No | Valid coordinate |
| Address Type | Home, Work, Other | Yes | Supported values only |
| Default Address | Indicates default delivery address | No | Boolean value |

#### 3.5.3.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to select a delivery address.
2. The selected delivery address shall belong to the authenticated customer.
3. Only active customer addresses shall be available for selection.
4. The system shall display all active saved delivery addresses associated with the customer account.
5. The customer shall be allowed to add a new delivery address during checkout.
6. The customer shall be allowed to edit an existing address before selecting it.
7. Deleted or inactive addresses shall not be displayed.
8. Only one delivery address shall be selected for a checkout session.
9. The selected address shall be associated with the current checkout session.
10. The delivery address shall be validated against the restaurant's serviceable delivery area.
11. Orders shall not proceed if the selected address is outside the delivery zone.
12. Delivery charges shall be calculated based on the selected address.
13. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated whenever the delivery address changes.
14. Delivery distance shall be calculated using the configured location service.
15. The system shall support multiple address types such as Home, Work, and Other.
16. Default delivery address shall be identified separately.
17. Customers shall be allowed to change the selected delivery address until the order is confirmed.
18. Any address modification shall immediately update the checkout session.
19. Address changes shall trigger recalculation of delivery charges.
20. Address changes shall trigger recalculation of estimated delivery time.
21. Delivery instructions shall remain associated with the selected address.
22. The system shall prevent duplicate address selection requests from creating inconsistent checkout data.
23. Invalid postal codes shall not be accepted.
24. Unsupported cities or delivery regions shall be rejected.
25. GPS coordinates, when available, shall be used to improve delivery accuracy.
26. Address validation shall occur before allowing progression to payment.
27. Customers shall not access addresses belonging to other customers.
28. Delivery eligibility shall always be verified using the latest restaurant configuration.
29. Selecting a delivery address shall not create an order.
30. No payment transaction shall be initiated during this use case.
31. The system shall maintain complete audit history for address selection and modifications.
32. The checkout session shall expire according to the configured timeout policy.
33. All address information shall comply with configured data validation rules.
34. The system shall preserve the selected address throughout the active checkout session.
35. The system shall maintain data consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.3.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify customer profile existence.
6. Verify selected address exists.
7. Verify selected address belongs to the authenticated customer.
8. Verify address is active.
9. Verify recipient name is provided.
10. Verify recipient mobile number format.
11. Verify house or flat number is provided.
12. Verify street address is provided.
13. Verify city is supported.
14. Verify state is supported.
15. Verify postal code format.
16. Verify postal code is serviceable.
17. Verify delivery area eligibility.
18. Verify restaurant delivers to the selected location.
19. Verify GPS coordinates, if provided.
20. Verify address type is valid.
21. Verify delivery charge calculation.
22. Verify estimated delivery time calculation.
23. Verify address update permissions.
24. Verify checkout session has not expired.
25. Verify Address Management Service availability.
26. Verify Delivery Service availability.
27. Verify Geo Location Service availability.
28. Verify all mandatory address fields are present before proceeding to the next checkout step.

#### 3.5.3.8 Main Flow

1. Customer navigates to the **Select Delivery Address** page during checkout.
2. System validates the customer's authentication and active checkout session.
3. System retrieves all active delivery addresses associated with the customer's account.
4. System validates ownership of each retrieved address.
5. System displays the list of available delivery addresses.
6. System identifies the customer's default delivery address, if configured.
7. Customer reviews the available delivery addresses.
8. Customer selects an existing delivery address or chooses to add a new address.
9. If a new address is selected, the system captures and validates the address details.
10. System verifies that all mandatory address fields are provided.
11. System validates the postal code.
12. System validates the city and state.
13. System validates GPS coordinates, if available.
14. System verifies that the selected address belongs to the restaurant's serviceable delivery area.
15. System calculates the delivery distance.
16. System calculates the applicable delivery charges.
17. System estimates the delivery time.
18. System retrieves the latest delivery configuration.
19. System updates the checkout session with the selected delivery address.
20. System recalculates delivery-related charges based on the selected address.
21. System updates the estimated delivery time.
22. System displays the finalized delivery address details.
23. System displays the updated delivery charges.
24. System displays the updated estimated delivery time.
25. Customer reviews the updated delivery information.
26. Customer confirms the selected delivery address.
27. System saves the confirmed delivery address for the current checkout session.
28. System records the address selection activity in the audit logs.
29. System refreshes the checkout summary with the latest delivery information.
30. Customer proceeds to the next checkout step.

---

#### 3.5.3.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: No Saved Delivery Address**

1. System finds that the customer has no saved delivery addresses.
2. System prompts the customer to add a new delivery address.
3. Customer enters the required address details.
4. System validates and saves the new address.
5. Customer continues with checkout.

---

**AF-2: Selected Address Outside Delivery Area**

1. System determines that the selected address is outside the restaurant's delivery zone.
2. System displays an appropriate message.
3. Customer selects another delivery address.
4. System revalidates the newly selected address.

---

**AF-3: Invalid Postal Code**

1. Customer enters an invalid postal code.
2. System displays a validation error.
3. Customer corrects the postal code.
4. Validation is performed again.

---

**AF-4: Address Deleted During Checkout**

1. System detects that the selected address has been deleted.
2. System informs the customer.
3. Customer selects another available address.
4. Checkout session is updated accordingly.

---

**AF-5: Delivery Charges Updated**

1. System detects changes in delivery pricing.
2. Delivery charges are recalculated.
3. Updated charges are displayed.
4. Customer reviews the revised checkout summary.

---

**AF-6: Estimated Delivery Time Updated**

1. Delivery availability changes.
2. System recalculates the estimated delivery time.
3. Updated delivery estimate is displayed.
4. Customer decides whether to continue.

---

**AF-7: Delivery Service Unavailable**

1. Delivery Service is temporarily unavailable.
2. System informs the customer.
3. Address validation cannot be completed.
4. Customer may retry later.

---

**AF-8: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Checkout session expires before address confirmation.
2. System displays a session expiration message.
3. Customer is redirected to the Shopping Cart page.
4. Customer initiates checkout again.

---

**AF-9: Duplicate Address Submission**

1. Customer submits the same address multiple times.
2. System identifies the duplicate request.
3. Only one address selection is processed.
4. Duplicate requests are ignored.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected system exception occurs.
2. System records the failure in application logs.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Customer may retry the operation after some time.

#### 3.5.3.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Select Delivery Address process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The selected delivery address shall be successfully associated with the current checkout session.
2. The selected address shall be validated as serviceable for the chosen restaurant.
3. Delivery distance shall be successfully calculated.
4. Delivery charges shall be calculated using the latest pricing configuration.
5. Estimated delivery time shall be generated based on the selected address.
6. The checkout summary shall be updated with the selected delivery address.
7. The latest delivery charges shall be reflected in the order summary.
8. The latest estimated delivery time shall be displayed to the customer.
9. The checkout session shall remain active until completion or timeout.
10. The customer shall be eligible to proceed to the next checkout step.

---

#### 3.5.3.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Select Delivery Address process, the system shall:

1. Display the selected delivery address.
2. Display the recipient name.
3. Display the recipient contact number.
4. Display the complete delivery location.
5. Display the calculated delivery distance.
6. Display the applicable delivery charges.
7. Display the estimated delivery time.
8. Update the checkout summary.
9. Save the selected address within the active checkout session.
10. Allow the customer to continue to the next checkout stage.
11. Record the successful address selection in the audit logs.
12. Preserve the selected address throughout the checkout process.
13. Refresh all delivery-related information automatically.

---

#### 3.5.3.12 Failure Response

If the Select Delivery Address process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Prevent progression to the next checkout stage.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Identify invalid address fields.
4. Notify the customer if the address is outside the delivery area.
5. Notify the customer if delivery is unavailable.
6. Reject unsupported postal codes.
7. Preserve previously selected valid address information whenever applicable.
8. Record failure details in application logs.
9. Maintain checkout session consistency.
10. Allow the customer to retry after resolving identified issues.

---

#### 3.5.3.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Select Delivery Address functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can select delivery addresses.
2. Checkout session is successfully validated.
3. Customer ownership of the selected address is verified.
4. Only active addresses are displayed.
5. Customers can add a new delivery address during checkout.
6. Customers can modify existing delivery addresses before confirmation.
7. Restaurant delivery zone is successfully validated.
8. Unsupported delivery locations are rejected.
9. Delivery charges are accurately calculated.
10. Estimated delivery time is accurately calculated.
11. Checkout summary is updated automatically after address selection.
12. Only one delivery address is associated with a checkout session.
13. Delivery address changes trigger recalculation of delivery charges.
14. Delivery address changes trigger recalculation of estimated delivery time.
15. Invalid postal codes are rejected.
16. Customer cannot access another customer's address.
17. Address selection activity is recorded in audit logs.
18. Checkout session integrity is maintained.
19. Customer can proceed only after selecting a valid delivery address.
20. All configured business rules are satisfied before progressing.

---

#### 3.5.3.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall access delivery address information.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before displaying saved addresses.
3. Customers shall only access addresses belonging to their own account.
4. All communications shall use secure HTTPS connections.
5. Address information shall be protected against unauthorized access.
6. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall not be exposed in URLs or application logs.
7. All address inputs shall be validated to prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), and other injection attacks.
8. Checkout Session IDs shall be securely validated before processing address selection.
9. Delivery address modifications shall be performed only within an active authenticated session.
10. Security-related events shall be recorded for auditing purposes.

### 3.5.4 Add New Delivery Address

#### 3.5.4.1 Description

The Add New Delivery Address use case enables the customer to create and save a new delivery address during the checkout process. The customer shall provide all mandatory address details including recipient information, contact number, complete delivery location, postal code, and address type.

The system shall validate the entered address, verify that the location falls within the restaurant's serviceable delivery area, and save the address to the customer's account. Upon successful creation, the newly added address shall automatically become available for selection and may be assigned as the delivery address for the current checkout session.

If the entered address is invalid or outside the supported delivery zone, the system shall notify the customer and prevent the address from being saved until all validation requirements are satisfied.

---

#### 3.5.4.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Customer Management Service
- Address Management Service
- Checkout Service
- Restaurant Service
- Delivery Service
- Geo Location Service
- Pricing Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.4.3 Preconditions

Before adding a new delivery address, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. Customer profile shall exist.
7. Customer shall have permission to modify delivery addresses.
8. Address Management Service shall be operational.
9. Delivery Service shall be available.
10. Geo Location Service shall be available.
11. Restaurant delivery configuration shall be available.
12. Pricing Service shall be operational.
13. Customer shall have available address storage capacity as per configured limits.
14. Network connectivity shall be available.
15. Required configuration data shall be loaded.
16. Restaurant shall be accepting orders.
17. System configuration for supported delivery locations shall be available.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.4.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer selects the **"Add New Delivery Address"** option during the checkout process.

---

#### 3.5.4.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Recipient Name | Name of the recipient | Yes | Alphabetic characters and supported symbols only |
| Mobile Number | Recipient contact number | Yes | Valid mobile number |
| Alternate Mobile Number | Secondary contact number | No | Valid mobile number |
| House/Flat Number | House, apartment, or building number | Yes | Cannot be empty |
| Street Address | Street and locality details | Yes | Maximum configured length |
| Landmark | Nearby landmark | No | Maximum configured length |
| Area/Locality | Area or locality | Yes | Cannot be empty |
| City | Delivery city | Yes | Supported city |
| State | Delivery state | Yes | Supported state |
| Postal Code | Delivery postal/ZIP code | Yes | Serviceable postal code |
| Latitude | GPS latitude | No | Valid coordinate |
| Longitude | GPS longitude | No | Valid coordinate |
| Address Type | Home, Work, Other | Yes | Supported values only |
| Default Address | Set as default delivery address | No | Boolean value |
| Delivery Instructions | Additional delivery notes | No | Maximum configured length |

#### 3.5.4.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to add a new delivery address.
2. The new delivery address shall be associated only with the authenticated customer's account.
3. All mandatory address fields shall be completed before the address can be saved.
4. Recipient name shall comply with the configured character validation rules.
5. Mobile number shall be unique within the customer's saved addresses where configured.
6. The postal code shall belong to a supported delivery region.
7. The delivery address shall fall within the restaurant's serviceable delivery area.
8. GPS coordinates, when available, shall be used to improve delivery accuracy.
9. The system shall validate the delivery location using the configured Geo Location Service.
10. Duplicate delivery addresses shall not be created for the same customer.
11. Customers shall be allowed to save multiple delivery addresses up to the configured system limit.
12. The customer may optionally designate the new address as the default delivery address.
13. If a new default address is selected, the previous default address shall automatically be updated.
14. Address type shall be limited to supported values such as Home, Work, and Other.
15. Delivery instructions shall remain optional.
16. Newly created addresses shall immediately become available for selection during checkout.
17. The checkout session shall automatically update when the customer selects the newly created address.
18. Delivery charges shall be recalculated after selecting the newly added address.
19. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated after selecting the newly added address.
20. Address validation shall occur before saving the address.
21. Invalid addresses shall not be persisted in the database.
22. Unsupported cities, states, or postal codes shall be rejected.
23. Customers shall not create addresses outside supported operating regions.
24. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be securely stored according to organizational policies.
25. Address creation shall not initiate payment processing.
26. Address creation shall not create an order.
27. All successful address creation events shall be recorded in audit logs.
28. Failed address creation attempts shall also be logged.
29. The checkout session shall remain active after successful address creation.
30. Address data shall be stored using standardized formatting rules.
31. Address information shall remain editable until the order is confirmed.
32. The system shall maintain referential integrity between customer and address records.
33. Duplicate submission requests shall be handled idempotently.
34. Address creation shall comply with all configured validation rules before persistence.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.4.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify customer profile existence.
6. Verify recipient name.
7. Verify primary mobile number format.
8. Verify alternate mobile number format, if provided.
9. Verify house or flat number.
10. Verify street address.
11. Verify locality or area.
12. Verify city.
13. Verify state.
14. Verify postal code format.
15. Verify postal code is serviceable.
16. Verify delivery zone eligibility.
17. Verify GPS coordinates, if provided.
18. Verify address type.
19. Verify duplicate address does not already exist.
20. Verify configured maximum address limit has not been exceeded.
21. Verify Delivery Service availability.
22. Verify Geo Location Service availability.
23. Verify Address Management Service availability.
24. Verify restaurant delivers to the selected location.
25. Verify delivery charge calculation.
26. Verify estimated delivery time calculation.
27. Verify all mandatory fields before saving the address.
28. Verify successful persistence of the new address before proceeding.

#### 3.5.4.8 Main Flow

1. Customer selects the **"Add New Delivery Address"** option during the checkout process.
2. System validates the customer's authentication and active checkout session.
3. System displays the Add New Delivery Address form.
4. Customer enters the recipient name.
5. Customer enters the primary mobile number.
6. Customer optionally enters an alternate mobile number.
7. Customer enters the house or flat number.
8. Customer enters the street address.
9. Customer optionally enters a landmark.
10. Customer enters the area or locality.
11. Customer enters the city.
12. Customer enters the state.
13. Customer enters the postal code.
14. Customer optionally allows GPS location access.
15. System retrieves the latitude and longitude, if available.
16. Customer selects the address type (Home, Work, or Other).
17. Customer optionally marks the address as the default delivery address.
18. Customer optionally enters delivery instructions.
19. Customer submits the address details.
20. System validates all mandatory input fields.
21. System validates the delivery location using the Geo Location Service.
22. System verifies that the address is within the restaurant's serviceable delivery area.
23. System checks for duplicate addresses.
24. System saves the new delivery address.
25. System updates the customer's saved address list.
26. System automatically makes the newly added address available for selection.
27. Customer selects the newly created delivery address.
28. System recalculates delivery charges and estimated delivery time.
29. System updates the checkout session with the selected address.
30. Customer proceeds to the next checkout step.

---

#### 3.5.4.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Mandatory Fields Missing**

1. Customer submits the form without completing all mandatory fields.
2. System identifies the missing fields.
3. Validation messages are displayed.
4. Customer completes the required information.
5. Address validation resumes.

---

**AF-2: Invalid Mobile Number**

1. Customer enters an invalid mobile number.
2. System rejects the input.
3. Validation message is displayed.
4. Customer corrects the mobile number.
5. Validation continues.

---

**AF-3: Unsupported Postal Code**

1. Customer enters a postal code outside the supported delivery region.
2. System identifies the unsupported location.
3. Customer is informed that delivery is unavailable.
4. Customer updates the postal code or selects another location.

---

**AF-4: Address Outside Delivery Zone**

1. System validates the entered address.
2. The address is determined to be outside the restaurant's delivery area.
3. System displays an appropriate error message.
4. Address is not saved.
5. Customer modifies the address details.

---

**AF-5: Duplicate Address Detected**

1. System identifies an existing address with identical details.
2. System informs the customer.
3. Customer may use the existing address instead.
4. Duplicate address creation is prevented.

---

**AF-6: Maximum Address Limit Reached**

1. Customer has reached the configured maximum number of saved addresses.
2. System displays the configured limit.
3. Customer deletes an existing address or cancels the operation.
4. Address creation is postponed until capacity is available.

---

**AF-7: Geo Location Service Unavailable**

1. GPS or Geo Location Service is temporarily unavailable.
2. System continues with manual address validation.
3. Address is validated using available location information.
4. Customer proceeds if validation succeeds.

---

**AF-8: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Checkout session expires during address creation.
2. System displays a session expiration message.
3. Newly entered information is discarded or temporarily cached according to system configuration.
4. Customer is redirected to restart checkout.

---

**AF-9: Database Save Failure**

1. System validates all address information successfully.
2. Database persistence fails unexpectedly.
3. System logs the failure.
4. Customer receives an appropriate error message.
5. Customer may retry the operation.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception in application logs.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. No incomplete address record is stored.
5. Customer may retry after the issue is resolved.

#### 3.5.4.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Add New Delivery Address process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The new delivery address shall be successfully created and stored in the customer's account.
2. The address shall be uniquely associated with the authenticated customer.
3. The newly created address shall be available for future orders.
4. The newly created address shall be available for immediate selection during the current checkout session.
5. If configured, the new address shall become the customer's default delivery address.

12. The customer shall be eligible to continue to the next checkout step.

Upon successful completion of the Add New Delivery Address process, the system shall:

2. Display the newly created delivery address.

4. Display the complete formatted delivery address.

13. Allow the customer to continue to the next checkout stage.

---

#### 3.5.4.12 Failure Response

If the Add New Delivery Address process cannot be completed, the system shall:

2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Highlight invalid input fields.
4. Notify the customer if the address is outside the supported delivery area.
5. Notify the customer if the postal code is not serviceable.
6. Reject duplicate delivery addresses.
7. Preserve valid user-entered information whenever possible.
8. Prevent incomplete or invalid address records from being stored.
9. Record failure details in the application logs.
10. Maintain checkout session consistency.
11. Allow the customer to correct the information and retry the operation.

5. Mobile number validation is performed successfully.

### 3.5.5 Edit Delivery Address

#### 3.5.5.1 Description

The Edit Delivery Address use case enables the customer to modify an existing delivery address associated with their account during the checkout process. The customer may update recipient information, contact numbers, address details, delivery instructions, address type, or default address preferences.

The system shall validate the modified address, verify that it remains within the restaurant's serviceable delivery area, and update the address information upon successful validation. If the edited address is currently selected for the active checkout session, the system shall automatically update the checkout session, recalculate delivery charges, estimate the delivery time, and refresh the order summary.

If the updated address becomes invalid or falls outside the supported delivery area, the system shall prevent the update and notify the customer to provide a valid serviceable address.

---

#### 3.5.5.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Customer Management Service
- Address Management Service
- Checkout Service
- Restaurant Service
- Delivery Service
- Geo Location Service
- Pricing Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.5.3 Preconditions

Before editing a delivery address, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Customer profile shall exist.
6. The selected delivery address shall exist.
7. The delivery address shall belong to the authenticated customer.
8. The delivery address shall be active.
9. Address Management Service shall be operational.
10. Delivery Service shall be available.
11. Geo Location Service shall be available.
12. Restaurant delivery configuration shall be available.
13. Pricing Service shall be operational.
14. Customer shall have permission to modify the selected address.
15. Restaurant shall be accepting orders.
16. Network connectivity shall be available.
17. Required configuration data shall be loaded.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.5.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer selects the **"Edit Delivery Address"** option for an existing saved delivery address during the checkout process.

---

#### 3.5.5.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Address ID | Existing delivery address identifier | Yes | Must exist and belong to customer |
| Recipient Name | Updated recipient name | Yes | Alphabetic characters and supported symbols only |
| Mobile Number | Updated primary contact number | Yes | Valid mobile number |
| Alternate Mobile Number | Updated secondary contact number | No | Valid mobile number |
| House/Flat Number | Updated house or apartment number | Yes | Cannot be empty |
| Street Address | Updated street and locality | Yes | Maximum configured length |
| Landmark | Updated nearby landmark | No | Maximum configured length |
| Area/Locality | Updated area or locality | Yes | Cannot be empty |
| City | Updated city | Yes | Supported city |
| State | Updated state | Yes | Supported state |
| Postal Code | Updated postal/ZIP code | Yes | Serviceable postal code |
| Latitude | Updated GPS latitude | No | Valid coordinate |
| Longitude | Updated GPS longitude | No | Valid coordinate |
| Address Type | Home, Work, Other | Yes | Supported values only |
| Default Address | Set as default delivery address | No | Boolean value |
| Delivery Instructions | Updated delivery notes | No | Maximum configured length |

#### 3.5.5.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to edit delivery addresses.
2. The delivery address being edited shall belong to the authenticated customer.
3. Only active delivery addresses shall be editable.
4. System-defined or protected addresses, if configured, shall not be editable.
5. All mandatory address fields shall be completed before the updated address can be saved.
6. Recipient name shall comply with the configured character validation rules.
7. Primary mobile number shall follow the configured mobile number format.
8. Alternate mobile number, if provided, shall follow the configured mobile number format.
9. The updated postal code shall belong to a supported delivery region.
10. The updated address shall fall within the restaurant's serviceable delivery area.
11. GPS coordinates, when available, shall be used to improve delivery accuracy.
12. The system shall validate the updated location using the configured Geo Location Service.
13. Duplicate delivery addresses shall not be created after editing.
14. Customers shall be allowed to change the address type (Home, Work, or Other).
15. Customers may update the default delivery address preference.
16. If the edited address is set as the default address, the previous default address shall automatically be updated.
17. Delivery instructions may be modified during address editing.
18. If the edited address is currently selected for checkout, the checkout session shall be updated automatically.
19. Delivery charges shall be recalculated whenever the edited address affects delivery distance or delivery zone.
20. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated after a successful address update.
21. The checkout summary shall automatically refresh after updating the address.
22. Invalid addresses shall not be persisted in the database.
23. Unsupported cities, states, or postal codes shall be rejected.
24. Customers shall not modify addresses belonging to other customers.
25. Address updates shall not initiate payment processing.
26. Address updates shall not create an order.
27. Successful address updates shall be recorded in the audit logs.
28. Failed address update attempts shall also be recorded.
29. Address updates shall maintain referential integrity across all dependent modules.
30. Duplicate update requests shall be processed idempotently.
31. Address information shall be stored using standardized formatting rules.
32. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be securely protected.
33. The checkout session shall remain active after a successful address update.
34. Address updates shall comply with all configured business validation rules.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.5.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify customer profile existence.
6. Verify address existence.
7. Verify address ownership.
8. Verify address is active.
9. Verify recipient name.
10. Verify primary mobile number format.
11. Verify alternate mobile number format, if provided.
12. Verify house or flat number.
13. Verify street address.
14. Verify locality or area.
15. Verify city.
16. Verify state.
17. Verify postal code format.
18. Verify postal code is serviceable.
19. Verify restaurant delivery eligibility.
20. Verify GPS coordinates, if provided.
21. Verify address type.
22. Verify duplicate address does not already exist after modification.
23. Verify Delivery Service availability.
24. Verify Geo Location Service availability.
25. Verify Address Management Service availability.
26. Verify delivery charge recalculation.
27. Verify estimated delivery time recalculation.
28. Verify successful persistence of the updated address before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.5.8 Main Flow

1. Customer navigates to the **Edit Delivery Address** page during the checkout process.
2. System validates the customer's authentication and active checkout session.
3. System retrieves the selected delivery address.
4. System verifies that the address belongs to the authenticated customer.
5. System displays the existing delivery address details.
6. Customer reviews the current address information.
7. Customer updates one or more editable fields.
8. Customer optionally changes the recipient name.
9. Customer optionally updates the primary or alternate mobile number.
10. Customer modifies the house or flat number, street address, landmark, locality, city, state, or postal code as required.
11. Customer optionally changes the address type.
12. Customer optionally updates delivery instructions.
13. Customer optionally marks the address as the default delivery address.
14. Customer submits the updated address information.
15. System validates all mandatory input fields.
16. System validates the updated postal code and serviceable delivery region.
17. System validates the updated location using the Geo Location Service.
18. System verifies that the updated address is within the restaurant's delivery area.
19. System checks for duplicate delivery addresses.
20. System updates the delivery address in the Address Management Service.
21. If applicable, system updates the customer's default delivery address.
22. If the edited address is selected for the active checkout session, system updates the checkout session.
23. System recalculates delivery charges based on the updated address.
24. System recalculates the estimated delivery time.
25. System refreshes the checkout summary.
26. System displays the updated delivery address.
27. System displays the updated delivery charges and estimated delivery time.
28. System records the successful address update in the audit logs.
29. Customer reviews the updated checkout information.
30. Customer proceeds to the next checkout step.

---

#### 3.5.5.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Mandatory Fields Missing**

1. Customer submits the updated address with one or more mandatory fields missing.
2. System identifies the missing fields.
3. Validation messages are displayed.
4. Customer completes the required information.
5. Validation resumes.

---

**AF-2: Invalid Mobile Number**

1. Customer enters an invalid mobile number.
2. System rejects the updated value.
3. Validation message is displayed.
4. Customer corrects the mobile number.
5. Address validation continues.

---

**AF-3: Unsupported Postal Code**

1. Customer updates the postal code to an unsupported delivery region.
2. System determines that delivery is unavailable.
3. Customer is informed of the validation failure.
4. Customer modifies the address.

---

**AF-4: Updated Address Outside Delivery Zone**

1. System validates the updated address.
2. The address is found to be outside the restaurant's delivery area.
3. System displays an appropriate error message.
4. Updated address is not saved.
5. Customer modifies the address and retries.

---

**AF-5: Duplicate Address Detected**

1. System identifies that the updated address duplicates another saved address.
2. System informs the customer.
3. Customer reviews the existing address.
4. Duplicate update is prevented.

---

**AF-6: Address Modified by Another Session**

1. System detects that the address has been updated from another active session.
2. Latest address information is retrieved.
3. Customer is notified about the update.
4. Customer reviews and edits the latest version.

---

**AF-7: Geo Location Service Unavailable**

1. Geo Location Service is temporarily unavailable.
2. System performs validation using configured fallback mechanisms.
3. Address validation continues where possible.
4. Customer proceeds if validation succeeds.

---

**AF-8: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Checkout session expires during address editing.
2. System displays a session expiration message.
3. Unsaved modifications are discarded or temporarily cached according to system configuration.
4. Customer is redirected to restart checkout.

---

**AF-9: Database Update Failure**

1. All validations complete successfully.
2. Database update fails unexpectedly.
3. System records the failure in application logs.
4. Customer receives an appropriate error message.
5. Customer retries the update later.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception in application logs.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Original address remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the operation.

#### 3.5.5.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Edit Delivery Address process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The selected delivery address shall be successfully updated in the customer's account.
2. The updated address shall remain associated with the authenticated customer.
3. If configured, the updated address shall become the customer's default delivery address.
4. If the edited address is selected for the active checkout session, the checkout session shall be updated automatically.
5. Delivery zone validation shall be completed successfully.
6. Delivery distance shall be recalculated if the location has changed.
7. Delivery charges shall be recalculated using the latest pricing configuration.
8. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated based on the updated address.
9. The checkout summary shall be refreshed with the latest delivery information.
10. The updated address shall be available for future orders.
11. The checkout session shall remain active until completion or timeout.
12. The customer shall be eligible to continue to the next checkout step.

---

#### 3.5.5.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Edit Delivery Address process, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation message indicating that the delivery address has been successfully updated.
2. Display the updated delivery address details.
3. Display the updated recipient name and contact number.
4. Display the updated delivery location.
5. Display the selected address type.
6. Indicate whether the address is the default delivery address.
7. Refresh the customer's saved address list.
8. Update the checkout session if the edited address is currently selected.
9. Display the recalculated delivery charges.
10. Display the updated estimated delivery time.
11. Refresh the checkout summary automatically.
12. Record the successful update in the audit logs.
13. Allow the customer to continue with the checkout process.

---

#### 3.5.5.12 Failure Response

If the Edit Delivery Address process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Prevent the updated address from being saved.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Highlight invalid input fields.
4. Notify the customer if the updated address is outside the supported delivery area.
5. Notify the customer if the postal code is not serviceable.
6. Reject duplicate delivery addresses.
7. Preserve the original address information if the update fails.
8. Prevent invalid or incomplete address information from being stored.
9. Record failure details in the application logs.
10. Maintain checkout session consistency.
11. Allow the customer to correct the information and retry the operation.

---

#### 3.5.5.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Edit Delivery Address functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can edit delivery addresses.
2. Customer ownership of the selected address is successfully verified.
3. Only active delivery addresses can be edited.
4. Mandatory fields are validated before saving.
5. Recipient information is validated successfully.
6. Mobile number validation is completed successfully.
7. Postal code validation is completed successfully.
8. Restaurant delivery zone validation is completed successfully.
9. Duplicate delivery addresses are prevented.
10. Unsupported delivery locations are rejected.
11. Updated address information is persisted successfully.
12. Checkout session is updated automatically when required.
13. Delivery charges are recalculated after address modification.
14. Estimated delivery time is recalculated automatically.
15. Checkout summary is refreshed automatically.
16. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) is securely protected.
17. Address update activity is recorded in audit logs.
18. Checkout session integrity is maintained.
19. Customer can continue checkout after a successful address update.
20. All configured business rules are satisfied before completing the update.

---

#### 3.5.5.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to edit delivery addresses.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before allowing address modifications.
3. Customers shall only edit addresses belonging to their own account.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections.
5. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be encrypted or protected according to organizational security policies.
6. Sensitive customer information shall not be exposed in URLs or application logs.
7. All address inputs shall be validated to prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), and other injection attacks.
8. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing address updates.
9. Unauthorized modification attempts shall be blocked and logged.
10. All security-related events shall be recorded for auditing and compliance.

---

The system shall record the following audit events during the Edit Delivery Address process:

1. Address update request timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID.
4. Address ID.
5. Previous address values, where permitted by organizational audit policy.
6. Updated address values, where permitted by organizational audit policy.
7. Default address status change.

9. Delivery charge recalculation result.

11. Customer IP Address.
12. Device Type.
13. Application Version.
14. Address update status (Success/Failure).
15. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
16. Audit log creation timestamp.
17. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's retention policy.

The Edit Delivery Address functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. Customer authentication has already been completed.
2. A valid checkout session exists.
3. The delivery address belongs to the authenticated customer.
4. Address Management Service is operational.
5. Geo Location Service is available.
6. Restaurant delivery zones are configured accurately.
