### 3.5.6 Delete Delivery Address

#### 3.5.6.1 Description

The Delete Delivery Address use case enables the customer to permanently remove an existing saved delivery address from their account during the checkout process. Before deleting the address, the system shall verify that the address belongs to the authenticated customer and determine whether the address is currently being used in an active checkout session or by any pending orders.

If the address is currently associated with an active checkout session, the system shall require the customer to select another valid delivery address before completing the deletion. If the address is the customer's default delivery address, the system shall automatically assign another eligible address as the default or remove the default designation if no other addresses exist.

The system shall ensure that deleting a delivery address does not impact completed orders or historical delivery records. Upon successful deletion, the customer's saved address list shall be refreshed and the checkout session shall be updated accordingly.

---

#### 3.5.6.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Customer Management Service
- Address Management Service
- Checkout Service
- Restaurant Service
- Delivery Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.6.3 Preconditions

Before deleting a delivery address, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. Customer profile shall exist.
4. A valid checkout session shall exist, if checkout is in progress.
5. Checkout session shall not have expired.
6. The selected delivery address shall exist.
7. The selected delivery address shall belong to the authenticated customer.
8. The selected delivery address shall be active.
9. Customer shall have permission to delete the selected address.
10. Address Management Service shall be operational.
11. Checkout Service shall be available.
12. Database Management System shall be operational.
13. Session Management Service shall be available.
14. Network connectivity shall be available.
15. Required application configuration shall be loaded.
16. All dependent backend services shall be operational.
17. Customer shall satisfy all configured authorization rules.
18. System shall be capable of verifying address dependencies before deletion.

---

#### 3.5.6.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer selects the **"Delete Delivery Address"** option for an existing saved delivery address during the checkout process.

---

#### 3.5.6.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | No | Must be valid if checkout is active |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Address ID | Delivery address identifier | Yes | Must exist and belong to customer |
| Delete Confirmation | Customer confirmation to delete the address | Yes | Must be explicitly confirmed |
| Authentication Token | Customer authentication token | Yes | Must be valid |
| Reason for Deletion | Customer-provided reason | No | Maximum configured length |

#### 3.5.6.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to delete delivery addresses.
2. The delivery address being deleted shall belong to the authenticated customer.
3. Only active delivery addresses shall be eligible for deletion.
4. The system shall require explicit customer confirmation before permanently deleting an address.
5. Customers shall not delete delivery addresses belonging to other customer accounts.
6. The system shall verify whether the selected address is currently associated with an active checkout session.
7. If the selected address is associated with an active checkout session, the customer shall be required to select another valid delivery address before deletion.
8. The system shall verify whether the address is the customer's default delivery address.
9. If the default address is deleted and other addresses exist, another eligible address shall automatically become the default address.
10. If no other addresses exist, the default delivery address designation shall be removed.
11. Delivery addresses associated with completed orders shall not be physically removed from historical order records.
12. Historical orders shall continue to display the delivery address used at the time of order placement.
13. The deletion process shall not affect completed, cancelled, or refunded orders.
14. Delivery addresses referenced by pending orders shall not be deleted until the dependency is resolved.
15. The system shall verify address dependencies before performing deletion.
16. Soft delete shall be used where required by organizational data retention policies.
17. Deleted addresses shall not appear in the customer's active address list.
18. Deleted addresses shall not be available for future checkout sessions.
19. The checkout session shall be updated immediately after successful deletion.
20. Delivery charges shall be recalculated if the deleted address was selected during checkout.
21. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated after selecting a replacement address.
22. Checkout summary shall be refreshed automatically after address replacement.
23. Address deletion shall not initiate payment processing.
24. Address deletion shall not create an order.
25. Duplicate delete requests shall be processed idempotently.
26. Customers shall be informed about the consequences of deleting an address before confirmation.
27. Successful address deletion events shall be recorded in audit logs.
28. Failed deletion attempts shall also be recorded.
29. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be handled according to organizational security policies.
30. The system shall maintain referential integrity across all related modules.
31. Address deletion shall comply with configured data retention policies.
32. Deleted addresses shall not be recoverable by customers unless supported by organizational policy.
33. The checkout session shall remain valid after successful address replacement.
34. All configured business validation rules shall be satisfied before deletion.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.6.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify customer authorization to delete the selected address.
4. Verify checkout session validity, if checkout is active.
5. Verify customer profile existence.
6. Verify address existence.
7. Verify address ownership.
8. Verify address is active.
9. Verify delete confirmation has been explicitly provided.
10. Verify authentication token validity.
11. Verify address is not associated with an active pending order.
12. Verify address dependency with the active checkout session.
13. Verify replacement address selection if required.
14. Verify default address replacement logic.
15. Verify address is eligible for deletion according to business policies.
16. Verify database referential integrity.
17. Verify Address Management Service availability.
18. Verify Checkout Service availability.
19. Verify Session Management Service availability.
20. Verify Database Management System availability.
21. Verify deletion request has not already been processed.
22. Verify data retention policy compliance.
23. Verify audit logging service availability.
24. Verify replacement delivery charge calculation, if applicable.
25. Verify replacement estimated delivery time calculation.
26. Verify checkout summary refresh after deletion.
27. Verify successful persistence of the deletion operation.
28. Verify all configured business rules before completing the deletion process.

#### 3.5.6.8 Main Flow

1. Customer navigates to the **Manage Delivery Addresses** section during the checkout process.
2. System validates the customer's authentication and active session.
3. System retrieves all active delivery addresses associated with the customer's account.
4. System displays the saved delivery addresses.
5. Customer selects an existing delivery address to delete.
6. System verifies that the selected address belongs to the authenticated customer.
7. System checks whether the selected address is currently active.
8. System verifies whether the address is associated with an active checkout session.
9. System verifies whether the address is associated with any pending orders.
10. System determines whether the address is the customer's default delivery address.
11. System displays a confirmation dialog explaining the consequences of deleting the address.
12. Customer reviews the confirmation message.
13. Customer confirms the deletion request.
14. System validates the deletion request.
15. If required, system prompts the customer to select a replacement delivery address.
16. Customer selects a replacement delivery address.
17. System updates the checkout session with the replacement address.
18. System recalculates delivery charges.
19. System recalculates the estimated delivery time.
20. System refreshes the checkout summary.
21. System deletes (or soft deletes) the selected delivery address according to organizational policy.
22. System updates the customer's saved address list.
23. System automatically assigns a new default address if applicable.
24. System updates all related customer address records.
25. System records the deletion activity in the audit logs.
26. System displays a successful deletion confirmation.
27. System refreshes the delivery address list.
28. Customer reviews the updated address list.
29. Customer continues with the checkout process.
30. System maintains checkout session consistency throughout the operation.

---

#### 3.5.6.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Customer Cancels Deletion**

1. Customer selects the Delete option.
2. Confirmation dialog is displayed.
3. Customer selects **Cancel**.
4. System closes the confirmation dialog.
5. No address is deleted.

---

**AF-2: Address Used in Active Checkout**

1. System detects that the selected address is currently associated with the active checkout session.
2. System requests the customer to select another delivery address.
3. Customer selects a replacement address.
4. Checkout session is updated.
5. Address deletion continues.

---

**AF-3: Address Associated with Pending Order**

1. System detects that the address is linked to a pending order.
2. System blocks the deletion request.
3. Customer is informed that the address cannot currently be deleted.
4. No address is removed.

---

**AF-4: Default Address Deleted**

1. Customer deletes the default delivery address.
2. System identifies another eligible address.
3. New default address is assigned automatically.
4. Customer is notified of the update.

---

**AF-5: No Replacement Address Available**

1. Customer attempts to delete the only saved delivery address.
2. System informs the customer that another address is required for checkout.
3. Customer adds a new delivery address.
4. Deletion resumes after a replacement address is available.

---

**AF-6: Delete Request Already Processed**

1. System receives a duplicate delete request.
2. System detects that the address has already been deleted.
3. Duplicate request is ignored.
4. Customer receives the current address list.

---

**AF-7: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Checkout session expires before deletion completes.
2. System displays a session expiration message.
3. Deletion process is cancelled.
4. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-8: Database Deletion Failure**

1. System validates the deletion request.
2. Database operation fails unexpectedly.
3. System records the failure.
4. Customer receives an error message.
5. Original address remains available.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Service Unavailable**

1. Address deletion succeeds.
2. Audit Logging Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. System queues the audit event according to retry policy.
4. Customer is not affected.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception in application logs.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. No partial deletion occurs.
5. Customer may retry the operation later.

#### 3.5.6.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Delete Delivery Address process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The selected delivery address shall be permanently deleted or soft deleted according to the organization's data retention policy.
2. The deleted address shall no longer appear in the customer's active delivery address list.
3. The deleted address shall no longer be available for future checkout sessions.
4. If the deleted address was associated with the active checkout session, the replacement address shall become the active delivery address.
5. Delivery charges shall be recalculated based on the replacement delivery address.
6. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated.
7. The checkout summary shall be refreshed with the updated delivery information.
8. If the deleted address was the default delivery address, another eligible address shall become the default address where applicable.
9. Historical orders shall continue to retain the original delivery address information.
10. The checkout session shall remain active until completion or timeout.
11. Customer profile information shall remain consistent after deletion.
12. The customer shall be eligible to continue with the checkout process.

---

#### 3.5.6.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Delete Delivery Address process, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation message indicating that the delivery address has been successfully deleted.
2. Refresh the customer's saved delivery address list.
3. Remove the deleted address from all selectable delivery addresses.
4. Display the updated default delivery address, if applicable.
5. Display the replacement delivery address selected for the checkout session.
6. Display recalculated delivery charges.
7. Display the updated estimated delivery time.
8. Refresh the checkout summary automatically.
9. Record the successful deletion in the audit logs.
10. Preserve historical order information.
11. Maintain checkout session continuity.
12. Allow the customer to continue to the next checkout stage.

---

#### 3.5.6.12 Failure Response

If the Delete Delivery Address process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Prevent deletion of the delivery address.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Notify the customer if the address is associated with an active or pending order.
4. Notify the customer if a replacement delivery address is required.
5. Preserve the existing delivery address information.
6. Prevent accidental deletion of customer data.
7. Maintain checkout session consistency.
8. Record failure details in application logs.
9. Preserve historical delivery records.
10. Allow the customer to retry after resolving identified issues.
11. Prevent partial deletion of related customer records.

---

#### 3.5.6.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Delete Delivery Address functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can delete delivery addresses.
2. Customer ownership of the selected address is successfully verified.
3. Explicit deletion confirmation is required.
4. Addresses belonging to other customers cannot be deleted.
5. Active checkout dependencies are successfully validated.
6. Pending order dependencies are successfully validated.
7. Replacement delivery address selection is enforced when required.
8. Default delivery address reassignment is completed automatically.
9. Deleted addresses are removed from the customer's active address list.
10. Historical orders continue to preserve delivery address information.
11. Delivery charges are recalculated after replacement address selection.
12. Estimated delivery time is recalculated automatically.
13. Checkout summary is refreshed automatically.
14. Duplicate deletion requests are handled safely.
15. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) remains protected.
16. Address deletion activity is recorded in audit logs.
17. Checkout session integrity is maintained.
18. Customer can continue checkout after selecting a valid replacement address.
19. Data retention policies are enforced.
20. All configured business rules are satisfied before completing deletion.

---

#### 3.5.6.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to delete delivery addresses.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before allowing deletion.
3. Customers shall only delete addresses belonging to their own accounts.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections.
5. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational security policies.
6. Deleted address information shall remain protected within historical records.
7. Sensitive customer information shall not be exposed in URLs or application logs.
8. All deletion requests shall be validated to prevent unauthorized operations, SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), and other injection attacks.
9. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing deletion requests.
10. Unauthorized deletion attempts shall be blocked and recorded for security auditing.

---

#### 3.5.6.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit events during the Delete Delivery Address process:

1. Address deletion request timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID, if applicable.
4. Deleted Address ID.
5. Default address status before deletion.
6. Replacement Address ID, if applicable.
7. Dependency validation result.
8. Delivery charge recalculation result.
9. Estimated delivery time recalculation result.
10. Customer IP Address.
11. Device Type.
12. Application Version.
13. Address deletion status (Success/Failure).
14. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
15. Audit log creation timestamp.
16. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's retention policy.

---

#### 3.5.6.16 Assumptions

The Delete Delivery Address functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. Customer authentication has already been completed.
2. The selected delivery address belongs to the authenticated customer.
3. A valid checkout session exists if checkout is in progress.
4. Address Management Service is operational.
5. Checkout dependencies can be verified successfully.
6. Historical order records are maintained separately from active customer addresses.
7. Organizational data retention policies are configured.
8. Network connectivity is available throughout the deletion process.
9. Audit Logging Service is operational.
10. All dependent backend services are operational.

---

#### 3.5.6.17 Dependencies

The Delete Delivery Address functionality depends on the successful availability of the following components and services:

1. Authentication Service
2. Customer Management Service
3. Address Management Service
4. Checkout Service
5. Restaurant Management Service
6. Delivery Management Service
7. Session Management Service
8. Notification Service
9. Audit Logging Service
10. Database Management System
11. Order Management Service

14. Security Service

17. Dependency Validation Service

### 3.5.7 Select Delivery Type

#### 3.5.7.1 Description

The Select Delivery Type use case enables the customer to choose the preferred delivery option for the current order during the checkout process. The system shall display all delivery types supported by the selected restaurant and applicable to the customer's delivery location.

Typical delivery types may include **Standard Delivery**, **Express Delivery**, **Scheduled Delivery**, **Contactless Delivery**, and other organization-configured delivery options. The availability of each delivery type shall depend on factors such as restaurant configuration, delivery partner availability, customer location, service hours, product eligibility, and operational constraints.

Upon selecting a delivery type, the system shall recalculate delivery charges, estimated delivery time, applicable service fees, and any delivery-type-specific restrictions before updating the checkout summary.

If the selected delivery type is unavailable, the system shall notify the customer and require selection of another supported delivery option.

---

#### 3.5.7.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Delivery Management Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Pricing Service
- Delivery Partner Management Service
- Geo Location Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.7.3 Preconditions

Before selecting a delivery type, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. A valid delivery address shall already be selected.
7. The selected delivery address shall be serviceable.
8. Restaurant shall be operational.
9. Restaurant shall be accepting orders.
10. Delivery Management Service shall be operational.
11. Pricing Service shall be available.
12. Delivery partner availability information shall be accessible.
13. Restaurant delivery configuration shall be available.
14. Customer shall have permission to modify checkout details.
15. Geo Location Service shall be operational.
16. Required configuration data shall be loaded.
17. Network connectivity shall be available.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.7.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer selects the **"Delivery Type"** option during the checkout process or when the system requires the customer to choose a delivery option before proceeding to payment.

---

#### 3.5.7.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Delivery Address ID | Selected delivery address | Yes | Must be serviceable |
| Delivery Type | Selected delivery option | Yes | Must be supported by the restaurant |
| Delivery Preference | Immediate, Scheduled, Contactless, etc. | No | Supported values only |
| Scheduled Delivery Date | Preferred delivery date | Conditional | Must be a future valid date |
| Scheduled Delivery Time | Preferred delivery time slot | Conditional | Available delivery slot |
| Special Delivery Instructions | Additional delivery notes | No | Maximum configured length |
| Delivery Partner Preference | Preferred delivery partner (if supported) | No | Configured values only |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match active checkout session |

#### 3.5.7.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to select a delivery type.
2. The selected delivery type shall be associated with the authenticated customer's active checkout session.
3. Only delivery types configured for the selected restaurant shall be displayed.
4. Only delivery types available for the selected delivery address shall be displayed.
5. Delivery type availability shall be determined using the latest restaurant configuration.
6. Delivery type availability shall consider delivery partner capacity.
7. Delivery type availability shall consider restaurant operating hours.
8. Delivery type availability shall consider delivery distance.
9. Delivery type availability shall consider serviceable delivery zones.
10. The system shall allow only one delivery type per checkout session.
11. Standard Delivery shall be available only when supported by the restaurant.
12. Express Delivery shall be available only when operational capacity permits.
13. Scheduled Delivery shall be available only when future delivery slots exist.
14. Contactless Delivery shall be available only when supported by the restaurant and delivery partner.
15. Changing the delivery type shall automatically update the checkout session.
16. Delivery charges shall be recalculated whenever the delivery type changes.
17. Service fees shall be recalculated whenever the delivery type changes.
18. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated after selecting a delivery type.
19. Available delivery slots shall be refreshed whenever the delivery type changes.
20. Customers shall be allowed to modify the selected delivery type until the order is confirmed.
21. Previously selected scheduled delivery information shall be cleared if it is incompatible with the newly selected delivery type.
22. The checkout summary shall automatically refresh after delivery type selection.
23. Invalid or unavailable delivery types shall not be accepted.
24. Customers shall not bypass delivery type validation through client-side manipulation.
25. Delivery type selection shall not initiate payment processing.
26. Delivery type selection shall not create an order.
27. Delivery type selection shall comply with configured operational policies.
28. Delivery restrictions imposed by restaurants shall be enforced.
29. Successful delivery type selections shall be recorded in audit logs.
30. Failed delivery type selection attempts shall also be logged.
31. Delivery type configuration shall be retrieved dynamically without requiring customer intervention.
32. Duplicate selection requests shall be processed idempotently.
33. The checkout session shall remain active after successful delivery type selection.
34. All configured business validations shall be completed before updating the checkout session.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.7.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify selected delivery address exists.
6. Verify selected delivery address is serviceable.
7. Verify restaurant availability.
8. Verify restaurant is accepting orders.
9. Verify selected delivery type is supported by the restaurant.
10. Verify selected delivery type is available for the customer's location.
11. Verify delivery partner availability.
12. Verify delivery distance eligibility.
13. Verify serviceable delivery zone.
14. Verify scheduled delivery eligibility, if applicable.
15. Verify scheduled delivery date.
16. Verify scheduled delivery time slot.
17. Verify selected delivery slot availability.
18. Verify delivery preference compatibility.
19. Verify pricing calculation.
20. Verify delivery charge calculation.
21. Verify service fee calculation.
22. Verify estimated delivery time calculation.
23. Verify Delivery Management Service availability.
24. Verify Pricing Service availability.
25. Verify Restaurant Management Service availability.
26. Verify Session Management Service availability.
27. Verify duplicate delivery type requests.
28. Verify successful persistence of the selected delivery type before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.7.8 Main Flow

1. Customer navigates to the **Checkout** page.
2. System validates the customer's authentication and active checkout session.
3. System retrieves the selected restaurant details.
4. System retrieves the selected delivery address.
5. System determines the serviceable delivery zone.
6. System retrieves all delivery types supported by the restaurant.
7. System verifies delivery partner availability.
8. System filters unavailable delivery types based on operational constraints.
9. System displays all eligible delivery types to the customer.
10. Customer reviews the available delivery options.
11. Customer selects the preferred delivery type.
12. System validates the selected delivery type.
13. System verifies that the selected delivery type is available for the delivery address.
14. System verifies restaurant support for the selected delivery type.
15. System verifies delivery partner availability for the selected delivery type.
16. If Scheduled Delivery is selected, the system retrieves available delivery slots.
17. Customer selects the preferred delivery slot, if applicable.
18. System validates the selected delivery slot.
19. System recalculates the delivery charges.
20. System recalculates applicable service fees.
21. System recalculates the estimated delivery time.
22. System updates the checkout session with the selected delivery type.
23. System updates the checkout summary.
24. System refreshes pricing information.
25. System refreshes delivery information.
26. System records the delivery type selection in the audit logs.
27. System displays the updated checkout summary.
28. Customer reviews the updated delivery details.
29. Customer proceeds to the next checkout step.
30. System maintains checkout session consistency throughout the process.

---

#### 3.5.7.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Customer Changes Delivery Type**

1. Customer selects a different delivery type.
2. System validates the newly selected delivery type.
3. System recalculates pricing and delivery estimates.
4. Checkout summary is refreshed.
5. Customer continues checkout.

---

**AF-2: Selected Delivery Type Not Available**

1. Customer selects a delivery type.
2. System determines that the delivery type is unavailable.
3. System displays an appropriate message.
4. Customer selects another available delivery type.

---

**AF-3: Delivery Partner Unavailable**

1. System detects insufficient delivery partner availability.
2. Selected delivery type becomes unavailable.
3. Alternative delivery types are displayed.
4. Customer selects another delivery option.

---

**AF-4: Restaurant Stops Supporting Selected Delivery Type**

1. Restaurant configuration changes during checkout.
2. System detects the configuration update.
3. Selected delivery type is invalidated.
4. Customer is prompted to choose another supported delivery type.

---

**AF-5: Scheduled Delivery Slot Unavailable**

1. Customer selects Scheduled Delivery.
2. Selected delivery slot is no longer available.
3. System displays updated available slots.
4. Customer selects another slot.

---

**AF-6: Delivery Address Outside Supported Zone**

1. Customer changes the delivery address.
2. System recalculates eligible delivery types.
3. Previously selected delivery type becomes invalid.
4. Customer selects another available delivery type.

---

**AF-7: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Checkout session expires before delivery type selection is completed.
2. System displays a session expiration message.
3. Customer is redirected to restart the checkout process.

---

**AF-8: Pricing Service Unavailable**

1. Delivery type selection succeeds.
2. Pricing recalculation fails temporarily.
3. System informs the customer that pricing cannot be updated.
4. Checkout remains unchanged until pricing is successfully recalculated.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Service Unavailable**

1. Delivery type selection is completed successfully.
2. Audit Logging Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. System queues the audit event according to retry policy.
4. Customer continues without interruption.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout session remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the operation later.

#### 3.5.7.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Select Delivery Type process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The selected delivery type shall be successfully associated with the active checkout session.
2. The customer's delivery preference shall be updated in the checkout session.
3. The selected delivery type shall become the active delivery option for the order.
4. Delivery charges shall be recalculated based on the selected delivery type.
5. Applicable service fees shall be recalculated.
6. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated.
7. Available delivery slots shall be refreshed, where applicable.
8. The checkout summary shall display the updated delivery type.
9. Pricing information shall reflect the latest delivery charges.
10. The checkout session shall remain active and consistent.
11. The delivery type selection shall be available for subsequent checkout steps.
12. Customer shall be allowed to proceed to the payment stage.

---

#### 3.5.7.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Select Delivery Type process, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation indicating that the delivery type has been successfully selected.
2. Display the selected delivery type.
3. Display updated delivery charges.
4. Display updated service fees.
5. Display the revised estimated delivery time.
6. Display available delivery slot information, if applicable.
7. Refresh the checkout summary automatically.
8. Refresh the order pricing details.
9. Persist the selected delivery type in the active checkout session.
10. Record the successful selection in the audit logs.
11. Maintain checkout session continuity.
12. Allow the customer to continue to the payment process.

---

#### 3.5.7.12 Failure Response

If the Select Delivery Type process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the selected delivery type.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Inform the customer when the selected delivery type is unavailable.
4. Inform the customer if delivery partner capacity is unavailable.
5. Inform the customer if the selected address is outside the supported delivery zone.
6. Preserve the previously selected delivery type, if any.
7. Prevent inconsistent checkout updates.
8. Maintain checkout session integrity.
9. Record failure details in application logs.
10. Allow the customer to retry with another supported delivery type.
11. Prevent progression to payment until a valid delivery type is selected.

---

#### 3.5.7.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Select Delivery Type functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can select a delivery type.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully validated.
3. Only restaurant-supported delivery types are displayed.
4. Delivery types are filtered based on the selected delivery address.
5. Delivery partner availability is verified.
6. Restaurant operational constraints are enforced.
7. Delivery zone validation is performed successfully.
8. Only one delivery type can be selected for a checkout session.
9. Delivery charges are recalculated after delivery type selection.
10. Service fees are recalculated correctly.
11. Estimated delivery time is updated automatically.
12. Available delivery slots are refreshed when applicable.
13. Checkout summary reflects the selected delivery type.
14. Duplicate selection requests are handled safely.
15. Delivery type selection does not initiate payment.
16. Delivery type selection is recorded in audit logs.
17. Checkout session consistency is maintained.
18. Customers can modify the delivery type before order confirmation.
19. All configured business rules are enforced.
20. The customer can successfully proceed to the payment stage after selecting a valid delivery type.

---

#### 3.5.7.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to select a delivery type.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before updating the checkout session.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing requests.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections.
5. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational security policies.
6. Delivery configuration information shall not be exposed to unauthorized users.
7. Sensitive checkout information shall not be exposed in URLs or application logs.
8. All requests shall be validated to prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), parameter tampering, and other injection attacks.
9. Unauthorized attempts to modify delivery preferences shall be blocked.
10. Security-related events shall be logged for auditing purposes.

---

#### 3.5.7.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit events during the Select Delivery Type process:

1. Delivery type selection request timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID.
4. Selected Delivery Type.
5. Previous Delivery Type, if applicable.
6. Delivery Address ID.
7. Restaurant ID.
8. Delivery charge calculation result.
9. Service fee calculation result.
10. Estimated delivery time calculation result.
11. Customer IP Address.
12. Device Type.
13. Application Version.
14. Delivery type selection status (Success/Failure).
15. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
16. Audit log creation timestamp.
17. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's retention policy.

---

#### 3.5.7.16 Assumptions

The Select Delivery Type functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. Customer authentication has already been completed.
2. An active checkout session exists.
3. A valid delivery address has already been selected.
4. Restaurant delivery configurations are available.
5. Delivery partner availability information is current.
6. Pricing Service is operational.
7. Geo Location Service is operational.
8. Network connectivity is available throughout the operation.
9. Audit Logging Service is operational.
10. All dependent backend services are operational.

---

#### 3.5.7.17 Dependencies

The Select Delivery Type functionality depends on the successful availability of the following components and services:

1. Authentication Service
2. Checkout Service
3. Customer Management Service
4. Restaurant Management Service
5. Delivery Management Service
6. Delivery Partner Management Service
7. Pricing Service
8. Geo Location Service
9. Session Management Service

### 3.5.8 Schedule Delivery

#### 3.5.8.1 Description

The Schedule Delivery use case enables the customer to select a future date and time for the delivery of an order during the checkout process. Instead of requesting immediate delivery, the customer may choose an available delivery slot based on the restaurant's operating hours, delivery partner availability, delivery location, and organizational scheduling policies.

The system shall display only valid future delivery dates and available time slots that satisfy all operational constraints. The availability of delivery slots shall be dynamically determined based on restaurant capacity, delivery partner availability, serviceable delivery zones, holidays, maintenance windows, and predefined scheduling rules.

After the customer selects a delivery date and time slot, the system shall validate the selection, reserve the slot (where applicable), recalculate delivery estimates if necessary, update the checkout session, and refresh the order summary.

If the selected delivery slot becomes unavailable before order confirmation, the system shall notify the customer and request selection of another available slot.

---

#### 3.5.8.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Delivery Scheduling Service
- Delivery Management Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Delivery Partner Management Service
- Geo Location Service
- Pricing Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.8.3 Preconditions

Before scheduling a delivery, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one item.
6. A valid delivery address shall already be selected.
7. The selected delivery address shall be serviceable.
8. A delivery type supporting scheduled delivery shall already be selected.
9. The selected restaurant shall support scheduled deliveries.
10. Restaurant shall be operational.
11. Restaurant shall be accepting scheduled orders.
12. Delivery Scheduling Service shall be operational.
13. Delivery partner availability information shall be available.
14. Restaurant scheduling configuration shall be available.
15. Customer shall have permission to modify checkout details.
16. Required scheduling configuration shall be loaded.
17. Network connectivity shall be available.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.8.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer selects the **"Schedule Delivery"** option during checkout and chooses to receive the order at a future date and time.

---

#### 3.5.8.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Restaurant ID | Selected restaurant identifier | Yes | Must exist |
| Delivery Address ID | Selected delivery location | Yes | Must be serviceable |
| Delivery Type | Selected delivery option | Yes | Must support scheduled delivery |
| Scheduled Delivery Date | Preferred delivery date | Yes | Must be a valid future date |
| Scheduled Delivery Time Slot | Preferred delivery time window | Yes | Must be an available slot |
| Time Zone | Customer's delivery time zone | Yes | Must be supported |
| Special Delivery Instructions | Additional delivery instructions | No | Maximum configured length |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match active checkout session |

#### 3.5.8.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to schedule deliveries.
2. Scheduled delivery shall be available only if supported by the selected restaurant.
3. Scheduled delivery shall be available only for serviceable delivery locations.
4. Only future delivery dates shall be permitted.
5. Past dates and time slots shall not be selectable.
6. The system shall display only available delivery slots configured by the restaurant.
7. Delivery slot availability shall be determined using the latest operational data.
8. Delivery slot availability shall consider restaurant operating hours.
9. Delivery slot availability shall consider restaurant holidays and maintenance windows.
10. Delivery slot availability shall consider delivery partner availability.
11. Delivery slot availability shall consider delivery zone capacity.
12. Delivery slot availability shall consider maximum scheduled order capacity.
13. The system shall allow only one scheduled delivery slot per checkout session.
14. Customers shall be allowed to modify the scheduled delivery before order confirmation.
15. Changing the delivery date shall automatically refresh available delivery slots.
16. Changing the delivery slot shall automatically update the checkout session.
17. Delivery charges shall be recalculated if scheduling affects pricing.
18. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated after scheduling.
19. The checkout summary shall automatically refresh after successful scheduling.
20. Expired delivery slots shall not be available for selection.
21. Reserved delivery slots shall not exceed configured capacity limits.
22. The system shall validate delivery slot availability immediately before order confirmation.
23. If a selected delivery slot becomes unavailable, the customer shall be required to select another available slot.
24. Scheduled delivery selection shall not initiate payment processing.
25. Scheduled delivery selection shall not create an order.
26. Scheduled delivery shall comply with organizational scheduling policies.
27. Successful scheduling events shall be recorded in audit logs.
28. Failed scheduling attempts shall also be recorded.
29. Duplicate scheduling requests shall be processed idempotently.
30. Customers shall be notified whenever their selected slot becomes unavailable.
31. Scheduling rules shall be configurable without application deployment.
32. The checkout session shall remain active after successful scheduling.
33. All configured business validations shall be completed before updating the checkout session.
34. Scheduling information shall be preserved until modified or checkout expires.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.8.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify selected restaurant exists.
6. Verify restaurant supports scheduled delivery.
7. Verify restaurant is operational.
8. Verify restaurant is accepting scheduled orders.
9. Verify selected delivery address exists.
10. Verify selected delivery address is serviceable.
11. Verify selected delivery type supports scheduled delivery.
12. Verify scheduled delivery date is a future date.
13. Verify scheduled delivery date is not a blocked holiday.
14. Verify scheduled delivery time slot exists.
15. Verify selected delivery slot is currently available.
16. Verify delivery slot capacity has not been exceeded.
17. Verify delivery partner availability for the selected slot.
18. Verify delivery zone availability.
19. Verify pricing recalculation.
20. Verify delivery charge recalculation.
21. Verify estimated delivery time calculation.
22. Verify Delivery Scheduling Service availability.
23. Verify Restaurant Management Service availability.
24. Verify Delivery Management Service availability.
25. Verify Session Management Service availability.
26. Verify duplicate scheduling requests.
27. Verify audit logging service availability.
28. Verify successful persistence of the scheduled delivery information before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.8.8 Main Flow

1. Customer navigates to the **Checkout** page.
2. System validates the customer's authentication and active checkout session.
3. System retrieves the selected restaurant information.
4. System retrieves the selected delivery address.
5. System verifies that the restaurant supports scheduled delivery.
6. System verifies that the selected delivery type supports scheduling.
7. System retrieves the restaurant's delivery schedule configuration.
8. System retrieves available delivery dates.
9. System retrieves available delivery time slots for the selected delivery address.
10. System filters unavailable dates and delivery slots based on operational constraints.
11. System displays all eligible delivery dates and available time slots.
12. Customer reviews the available delivery schedule.
13. Customer selects the preferred delivery date.
14. Customer selects the preferred delivery time slot.
15. System validates the selected delivery date.
16. System validates the selected delivery time slot.
17. System verifies delivery partner availability for the selected slot.
18. System verifies delivery zone capacity.
19. System verifies restaurant order capacity for the selected slot.
20. System temporarily reserves the selected delivery slot, where applicable.
21. System recalculates delivery charges, if applicable.
22. System recalculates the estimated delivery time.
23. System updates the checkout session with the scheduled delivery information.
24. System refreshes the checkout summary.
25. System updates the order pricing details.
26. System records the scheduled delivery selection in the audit logs.
27. System displays the selected delivery date and time slot.
28. Customer reviews the updated checkout summary.
29. Customer proceeds to the next checkout step.
30. System maintains checkout session consistency throughout the scheduling process.

---

#### 3.5.8.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Customer Changes Scheduled Delivery Date**

1. Customer selects a different delivery date.
2. System retrieves available delivery slots for the newly selected date.
3. Previously displayed delivery slots are refreshed.
4. Customer selects a new available delivery slot.
5. Checkout session is updated successfully.

---

**AF-2: Selected Delivery Slot No Longer Available**

1. Customer selects a delivery slot.
2. System detects that the slot has become unavailable.
3. System informs the customer.
4. Updated available delivery slots are displayed.
5. Customer selects another available slot.

---

**AF-3: Restaurant Stops Accepting Scheduled Orders**

1. Restaurant scheduling configuration changes.
2. System detects that scheduled delivery is no longer available.
3. Scheduled delivery selection is cancelled.
4. Customer is prompted to select Immediate Delivery or another available option.

---

**AF-4: Delivery Partner Capacity Reached**

1. System detects that delivery partner capacity has been exhausted for the selected slot.
2. The selected slot becomes unavailable.
3. Alternative delivery slots are displayed.
4. Customer selects another available slot.

---

**AF-5: Delivery Zone Capacity Exceeded**

1. System determines that the selected delivery zone has reached its capacity.
2. Selected delivery slot is rejected.
3. Customer is notified.
4. Alternative delivery slots are displayed.

---

**AF-6: Customer Changes Delivery Address**

1. Customer updates the delivery address.
2. System recalculates available delivery dates and slots.
3. Previously selected schedule becomes invalid.
4. Customer selects a new delivery schedule.

---

**AF-7: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Checkout session expires before scheduling is completed.
2. System displays a session expiration message.
3. Scheduled delivery information is discarded.
4. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-8: Delivery Scheduling Service Unavailable**

1. System cannot retrieve delivery schedules.
2. Customer is informed that scheduled delivery is temporarily unavailable.
3. Immediate delivery remains available, if supported.
4. Customer may retry later.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Service Unavailable**

1. Scheduled delivery selection is completed successfully.
2. Audit Logging Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. System queues the audit event according to retry policy.
4. Customer continues checkout without interruption.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout session remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the scheduling operation later.

#### 3.5.8.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Schedule Delivery process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The selected delivery date shall be successfully associated with the active checkout session.
2. The selected delivery time slot shall be successfully associated with the active checkout session.
3. The scheduled delivery preference shall become the active delivery schedule for the order.
4. The selected delivery slot shall be reserved temporarily, where applicable, until order confirmation.
5. Delivery charges shall be recalculated if scheduling impacts pricing.
6. Estimated delivery time shall be recalculated based on the selected schedule.
7. The checkout summary shall display the scheduled delivery date and time.
8. The checkout session shall be updated with the latest scheduling information.
9. Pricing information shall reflect the updated delivery schedule.
10. The scheduled delivery information shall remain editable until order confirmation.
11. The checkout session shall remain active and consistent.
12. Customer shall be allowed to proceed to the payment stage.

---

#### 3.5.8.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Schedule Delivery process, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation indicating that the delivery schedule has been successfully saved.
2. Display the selected delivery date.
3. Display the selected delivery time slot.
4. Display updated delivery charges, if applicable.
5. Display the revised estimated delivery time.
6. Refresh the checkout summary automatically.
7. Persist the scheduled delivery information in the active checkout session.
8. Display any applicable scheduling restrictions or notes.
9. Record the successful scheduling event in the audit logs.
10. Maintain checkout session continuity.
11. Preserve the selected schedule until modified or order confirmation.
12. Allow the customer to continue to the payment process.

---

#### 3.5.8.12 Failure Response

If the Schedule Delivery process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the selected delivery schedule.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Inform the customer if the selected delivery slot is unavailable.
4. Inform the customer if the restaurant is not accepting scheduled deliveries.
5. Inform the customer if delivery partner capacity has been exceeded.
6. Inform the customer if the selected delivery date is invalid.
7. Preserve the previously selected delivery schedule, if any.
8. Maintain checkout session consistency.
9. Record failure details in application logs.
10. Allow the customer to retry using another available delivery slot.
11. Prevent progression to payment until a valid delivery schedule is selected.

---

#### 3.5.8.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Schedule Delivery functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can schedule deliveries.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully validated.
3. Only restaurants supporting scheduled delivery allow scheduling.
4. Only future delivery dates can be selected.
5. Past dates and expired delivery slots cannot be selected.
6. Available delivery slots are displayed dynamically.
7. Restaurant operating hours are enforced.
8. Delivery partner availability is validated.
9. Delivery zone capacity is validated.
10. Maximum scheduled order capacity is enforced.
11. Delivery charges are recalculated when applicable.
12. Estimated delivery time is updated automatically.
13. Checkout summary reflects the selected delivery schedule.
14. Customers can modify the schedule before order confirmation.
15. Duplicate scheduling requests are handled safely.
16. Scheduled delivery selection does not initiate payment.
17. Scheduling activity is recorded in audit logs.
18. Checkout session consistency is maintained.
19. All configured scheduling policies are enforced.
20. Customer can successfully proceed to the payment stage after selecting a valid delivery schedule.

---

#### 3.5.8.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to schedule deliveries.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before updating checkout information.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing requests.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections.
5. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational security policies.
6. Delivery schedule information shall not be exposed to unauthorized users.
7. Sensitive checkout information shall not be exposed in URLs or application logs.
8. All requests shall be validated to prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), parameter tampering, and other injection attacks.
9. Unauthorized scheduling modification attempts shall be blocked.
10. Security-related events shall be logged for auditing purposes.

---

#### 3.5.8.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit events during the Schedule Delivery process:

1. Schedule delivery request timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID.
4. Restaurant ID.
5. Delivery Address ID.
6. Selected delivery date.
7. Selected delivery time slot.
8. Previous delivery schedule, if applicable.
9. Delivery charge recalculation result.
10. Estimated delivery time calculation result.
11. Customer IP Address.
12. Device Type.
13. Application Version.
14. Schedule delivery status (Success/Failure).
15. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
16. Audit log creation timestamp.
17. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's retention policy.

---

#### 3.5.8.16 Assumptions

The Schedule Delivery functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. Customer authentication has already been completed.
2. An active checkout session exists.
3. A valid delivery address has already been selected.
4. The selected delivery type supports scheduled delivery.
5. Restaurant scheduling configuration is available.
6. Delivery partner availability information is current.
7. Pricing Service is operational.
8. Network connectivity is available throughout the scheduling process.
9. Audit Logging Service is operational.
10. All dependent backend services are operational.

---

#### 3.5.8.17 Dependencies

The Schedule Delivery functionality depends on the successful availability of the following components and services:

2. Checkout Service
3. Customer Management Service
4. Delivery Scheduling Service
5. Delivery Management Service
6. Delivery Partner Management Service

### 3.5.9 Select Payment Method

#### 3.5.9.1 Description

The Select Payment Method use case enables the customer to choose a preferred payment option for the current order during the checkout process. The system shall display all payment methods supported by the platform, the selected restaurant, the customer's location, and applicable business policies.

Supported payment methods may include **Credit Card**, **Debit Card**, **UPI**, **Net Banking**, **Digital Wallets**, **Cash on Delivery (COD)**, **Gift Card**, **Store Credits**, and other organization-configured payment options.

The availability of each payment method shall depend on factors such as restaurant preferences, order value, delivery location, payment gateway availability, customer eligibility, promotional restrictions, fraud prevention policies, and operational constraints.

After the customer selects a payment method, the system shall validate its availability, update the checkout session, calculate any applicable payment-related charges or discounts, and refresh the order summary.

Selection of a payment method shall not initiate payment authorization or payment capture. Actual payment processing shall occur only after the customer explicitly confirms the order.

---

#### 3.5.9.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Payment Management Service
- Payment Gateway
- Restaurant Management Service
- Pricing Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.9.3 Preconditions

Before selecting a payment method, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. A valid delivery address shall already be selected.
7. Delivery type shall already be selected.
8. Checkout summary shall be successfully generated.
9. Final payable amount shall be calculated.
10. Restaurant shall be operational.
11. Restaurant shall be accepting orders.
12. Payment Management Service shall be operational.
13. At least one supported payment method shall be available.
14. Pricing Service shall be operational.
15. Customer shall have permission to modify checkout details.
16. Required payment configuration shall be loaded.
17. Network connectivity shall be available.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.9.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer reaches the **Payment** section during checkout and selects a preferred payment method before placing the order.

---

#### 3.5.9.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Payment Method | Selected payment option | Yes | Must be supported |
| Order Amount | Total payable amount | Yes | Must match checkout summary |
| Currency | Transaction currency | Yes | Supported currency only |
| Payment Preference | Saved or new payment preference | No | Configured values only |
| Billing Address ID | Billing address identifier | Conditional | Must exist if applicable |
| Save Payment Method | Save selected payment method for future use | No | Boolean value |
| Device Information | Device details for fraud analysis | No | Valid format |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match active checkout session |

#### 3.5.9.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to select a payment method.
2. The selected payment method shall be associated with the authenticated customer's active checkout session.
3. Only payment methods configured by the platform shall be displayed.
4. Payment method availability shall depend on the selected restaurant's payment configuration.
5. Payment method availability shall depend on the customer's delivery location.
6. Payment method availability shall depend on the total payable order amount.
7. Cash on Delivery (COD) shall be available only for eligible restaurants and serviceable locations.
8. COD shall be restricted if the order value exceeds the configured maximum limit.
9. Gift Cards and Store Credits shall be available only if the customer has an eligible balance.
10. Payment Gateway availability shall be verified before displaying online payment methods.
11. The system shall allow only one primary payment method per checkout session.
12. Customers shall be allowed to change the selected payment method until the order is confirmed.
13. Changing the payment method shall automatically update the checkout session.
14. Applicable payment convenience fees shall be recalculated after payment method selection.
15. Applicable payment discounts or cashback offers shall be recalculated based on the selected payment method.
16. The final payable amount shall be refreshed after payment method selection.
17. The checkout summary shall automatically refresh after payment method selection.
18. Payment method selection shall not authorize payment.
19. Payment method selection shall not capture payment.
20. Payment method selection shall not create an order.
21. Payment authorization shall occur only after the customer confirms the order.
22. Customers shall not bypass payment eligibility validations through client-side manipulation.
23. Payment method configuration shall be retrieved dynamically.
24. Payment methods temporarily unavailable due to gateway outages shall not be selectable.
25. Fraud prevention policies shall be evaluated before accepting the selected payment method.
26. Duplicate payment method selection requests shall be processed idempotently.
27. Successful payment method selections shall be recorded in audit logs.
28. Failed payment method selection attempts shall also be recorded.
29. The checkout session shall remain active after successful payment method selection.
30. The selected payment method shall remain editable until order placement.
31. Payment configuration changes shall be reflected immediately without requiring application deployment.
32. The system shall comply with organizational payment security policies.
33. All configured business validations shall be completed before updating the checkout session.
34. Sensitive payment information shall never be stored in plain text.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.9.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify selected payment method exists.
6. Verify selected payment method is supported by the platform.
7. Verify selected payment method is supported by the restaurant.
8. Verify selected payment method is available for the customer's location.
9. Verify total payable amount.
10. Verify COD eligibility, if applicable.
11. Verify maximum COD order value limits.
12. Verify Gift Card eligibility, if applicable.
13. Verify Store Credit availability, if applicable.
14. Verify Payment Gateway availability.
15. Verify payment convenience fee calculation.
16. Verify payment discount calculation.
17. Verify final payable amount calculation.
18. Verify fraud detection policy compliance.
19. Verify Pricing Service availability.
20. Verify Payment Management Service availability.
21. Verify Payment Gateway connectivity.
22. Verify Restaurant Management Service availability.
23. Verify Session Management Service availability.
24. Verify duplicate payment method selection requests.
25. Verify audit logging service availability.
26. Verify configuration integrity for the selected payment method.
27. Verify secure transmission of payment selection data.
28. Verify successful persistence of the selected payment method before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.9.8 Main Flow

1. Customer navigates to the **Payment** section of the checkout process.
2. System validates the customer's authentication and active checkout session.
3. System retrieves the final checkout summary.
4. System retrieves the total payable amount.
5. System retrieves the payment configuration applicable to the selected restaurant.
6. System verifies Payment Gateway availability.
7. System determines eligible payment methods based on platform policies.
8. System evaluates payment method eligibility based on delivery location and order value.
9. System filters unavailable payment methods.
10. System displays all eligible payment methods.
11. Customer reviews the available payment options.
12. Customer selects the preferred payment method.
13. System validates the selected payment method.
14. System verifies that the selected payment method is currently available.
15. System verifies payment gateway support for the selected payment method.
16. System evaluates fraud prevention policies.
17. System calculates any applicable convenience fees.
18. System calculates applicable payment discounts or cashback offers.
19. System recalculates the final payable amount.
20. System updates the checkout session with the selected payment method.
21. System refreshes the checkout summary.
22. System updates the payment information displayed to the customer.
23. System securely stores the selected payment preference for the current checkout session.
24. If requested, the system securely stores the payment preference for future use without storing sensitive payment credentials.
25. System records the payment method selection in the audit logs.
26. System displays the updated payment summary.
27. Customer reviews the updated checkout details.
28. Customer confirms the selected payment method.
29. Customer proceeds to the **Place Order** step.
30. System maintains checkout session consistency throughout the process.

---

#### 3.5.9.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Customer Changes Payment Method**

1. Customer selects a different payment method.
2. System validates the newly selected payment method.
3. System recalculates applicable charges and discounts.
4. Checkout summary is refreshed.
5. Customer continues checkout.

---

**AF-2: Selected Payment Method Unavailable**

1. Customer selects a payment method.
2. System determines that the payment method is unavailable.
3. System displays an appropriate message.
4. Customer selects another available payment method.

---

**AF-3: Payment Gateway Unavailable**

1. System detects Payment Gateway unavailability.
2. Online payment methods are temporarily disabled.
3. Remaining eligible payment methods are displayed.
4. Customer selects another available payment option.

---

**AF-4: Cash on Delivery Not Eligible**

1. Customer selects Cash on Delivery.
2. System verifies COD eligibility.
3. COD is determined to be unavailable due to business policies.
4. System informs the customer.
5. Customer selects another supported payment method.

---

**AF-5: Gift Card or Store Credit Balance Insufficient**

1. Customer selects Gift Card or Store Credit.
2. System verifies available balance.
3. Available balance is insufficient.
4. System notifies the customer.
5. Customer selects another payment method or combines payment methods if supported.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Validation Failed**

1. Fraud Detection Service flags the selected payment method.
2. System blocks the payment method selection.
3. Customer receives a generic validation message.
4. Customer selects another eligible payment method.

---

**AF-7: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Checkout session expires before payment method selection is completed.
2. System displays a session expiration message.
3. Payment selection is discarded.
4. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-8: Pricing Updated Before Confirmation**

1. System detects pricing changes before payment method confirmation.
2. Checkout summary is recalculated.
3. Customer reviews the updated payable amount.
4. Customer re-confirms the payment method.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Service Unavailable**

1. Payment method selection succeeds.
2. Audit Logging Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. System queues the audit event according to retry policy.
4. Customer continues checkout without interruption.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout session remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the operation later.

#### 3.5.9.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Select Payment Method process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The selected payment method shall be successfully associated with the active checkout session.
2. The customer's payment preference shall be updated in the checkout session.
3. The selected payment method shall become the active payment option for the current order.
4. Applicable payment convenience fees shall be recalculated.
5. Applicable payment discounts, cashback, or promotional benefits shall be recalculated.
6. The final payable amount shall be updated based on the selected payment method.
7. The checkout summary shall display the updated payment information.
8. If requested, the customer's payment preference shall be securely saved for future use without storing sensitive payment credentials.
9. The checkout session shall remain active and consistent.
10. The selected payment method shall remain editable until the order is placed.
11. No payment authorization or payment capture shall occur during this use case.
12. Customer shall be allowed to proceed to the Place Order step.

---

#### 3.5.9.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Select Payment Method process, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation indicating that the payment method has been successfully selected.
2. Display the selected payment method.
3. Display updated convenience fees, if applicable.
4. Display updated payment discounts or cashback benefits.
5. Display the updated final payable amount.
6. Refresh the checkout summary automatically.
7. Persist the selected payment method in the active checkout session.
8. Display payment-related notes or eligibility conditions, if applicable.
9. Record the successful payment method selection in the audit logs.
10. Maintain checkout session continuity.
11. Preserve the selected payment method until modified or order placement.
12. Allow the customer to continue to the Place Order process.

---

#### 3.5.9.12 Failure Response

If the Select Payment Method process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the selected payment method.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Inform the customer if the selected payment method is unavailable.
4. Inform the customer if Payment Gateway services are temporarily unavailable.
5. Inform the customer if Cash on Delivery is not eligible.
6. Inform the customer if Gift Card or Store Credit balance is insufficient.
7. Preserve the previously selected payment method, if any.
8. Maintain checkout session consistency.
9. Record failure details in application logs.
10. Allow the customer to retry using another supported payment method.
11. Prevent progression to order placement until a valid payment method is selected.

---

#### 3.5.9.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Select Payment Method functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can select a payment method.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully validated.
3. Only supported payment methods are displayed.
4. Restaurant-specific payment restrictions are enforced.
5. Location-based payment restrictions are enforced.
6. Cash on Delivery eligibility is validated correctly.
7. Payment Gateway availability is verified.
8. Fraud prevention policies are evaluated successfully.
9. Payment convenience fees are calculated correctly.
10. Payment discounts and cashback offers are calculated correctly.
11. Final payable amount is updated automatically.
12. Checkout summary reflects the selected payment method.
13. Customers can modify the payment method before order placement.
14. Duplicate payment method selection requests are handled safely.
15. Payment method selection does not authorize payment.
16. Payment method selection does not capture payment.
17. Payment method selection is recorded in audit logs.
18. Checkout session consistency is maintained.
19. All configured business rules are enforced successfully.
20. Customer can successfully proceed to the Place Order step after selecting a valid payment method.

---

#### 3.5.9.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to select payment methods.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before updating checkout information.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing requests.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections with TLS encryption.
5. Sensitive payment credentials shall never be stored in plain text.
6. Payment information shall comply with PCI DSS requirements.
7. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational security policies.
8. Sensitive payment information shall not be exposed in URLs, client-side logs, or application logs.
9. All requests shall be validated to prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), parameter tampering, replay attacks, and other injection attacks.
10. Unauthorized attempts to modify payment preferences shall be blocked and recorded for security auditing.

---

#### 3.5.9.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit events during the Select Payment Method process:

1. Payment method selection request timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID.
4. Selected Payment Method.
5. Previous Payment Method, if applicable.
6. Order Amount.
7. Final Payable Amount.
8. Convenience fee calculation result.
9. Payment discount calculation result.
10. Fraud validation result.
11. Customer IP Address.
12. Device Type.
13. Application Version.
14. Payment method selection status (Success/Failure).
15. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
16. Audit log creation timestamp.
17. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's retention policy.

---

#### 3.5.9.16 Assumptions

The Select Payment Method functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. Customer authentication has already been completed.
2. An active checkout session exists.
3. Delivery address and delivery type have already been selected.
4. Checkout summary has been generated successfully.
5. Payment Management Service is operational.
6. Payment Gateway connectivity is available.
7. Fraud Detection Service is operational.
8. Pricing Service is operational.
9. Network connectivity is available throughout the payment selection process.
10. All dependent backend services are operational.
