### 3.5.10 Apply Coupon at Checkout

#### 3.5.10.1 Description

The Apply Coupon at Checkout use case enables the customer to apply a valid coupon, promotional code, discount voucher, referral code, or other promotional benefit to the current order before placing it. The system shall validate the coupon against configured business rules and determine whether it is eligible for the current checkout session.

Coupon eligibility shall be evaluated based on factors including coupon validity period, customer eligibility, restaurant eligibility, minimum order value, maximum discount limit, product restrictions, usage limits, geographic restrictions, payment method restrictions, delivery type restrictions, and promotional policies.

Upon successful validation, the applicable discount shall be calculated and applied to the order. The checkout summary shall be updated automatically to reflect the discount, revised taxes (where applicable), delivery charges (if affected), service fees, and the final payable amount.

If the coupon is invalid, expired, already used, exceeds usage limits, or violates any configured business rule, the system shall reject the coupon and provide an appropriate validation message without affecting the existing checkout session.

---

#### 3.5.10.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Coupon Management Service
- Promotion Engine
- Pricing Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Payment Management Service
- Customer Management Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.10.3 Preconditions

Before applying a coupon during checkout, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. A valid delivery address shall already be selected.
7. Delivery type shall already be selected.
8. Payment method shall already be selected, if required by coupon rules.
9. Checkout summary shall be successfully generated.
10. Final order amount shall be available.
11. Coupon Management Service shall be operational.
12. Promotion Engine shall be operational.
13. Pricing Service shall be operational.
14. Restaurant shall be operational.
15. Customer shall have permission to modify checkout details.
16. Required promotional configuration shall be loaded.
17. Network connectivity shall be available.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.10.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer enters a coupon or promotional code in the **Coupon/Promo Code** section during checkout and selects the **Apply** option.

---

#### 3.5.10.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Coupon Code | Promotional code entered by the customer | Yes | Must exist and be active |
| Order Amount | Current order subtotal | Yes | Must match checkout summary |
| Restaurant ID | Selected restaurant identifier | Yes | Must exist |
| Payment Method | Selected payment method | Conditional | Must satisfy coupon eligibility rules |
| Delivery Type | Selected delivery option | Conditional | Must satisfy coupon eligibility rules |
| Customer Segment | Customer category (if applicable) | No | Configured values only |
| Device Information | Device details for fraud analysis | No | Valid format |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match active checkout session |

#### 3.5.10.8 Main Flow

1. Customer navigates to the **Checkout** page.
2. System validates the customer's authentication and active checkout session.
3. System retrieves the latest checkout summary.
4. System retrieves the current order subtotal.
5. System displays the **Coupon / Promo Code** section.
6. Customer enters a coupon or promotional code.
7. Customer clicks the **Apply** button.
8. System validates the coupon code format.
9. System verifies that the coupon exists.
10. System verifies that the coupon is active.
11. System verifies the coupon validity period.
12. System verifies customer eligibility.
13. System verifies restaurant eligibility.
14. System verifies product eligibility.
15. System verifies payment method eligibility, if applicable.
16. System verifies delivery type eligibility, if applicable.
17. System validates the minimum order value requirement.
18. System validates customer usage limits.
19. System validates global coupon usage limits.
20. System calculates the applicable discount.
21. System enforces the maximum discount limit.
22. System recalculates applicable taxes.
23. System recalculates delivery charges, if applicable.
24. System recalculates service fees, if applicable.
25. System recalculates the final payable amount.
26. System updates the checkout session with the applied coupon.
27. System refreshes the checkout summary.
28. System records the coupon application in the audit logs.
29. System displays the applied coupon and updated order summary.
30. Customer reviews the updated pricing and proceeds with checkout.

---

#### 3.5.10.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Invalid Coupon Code**

1. Customer enters an invalid coupon code.
2. System validates the coupon.
3. Coupon is not found.
4. System displays an "Invalid Coupon Code" message.
5. Customer enters another coupon.

---

**AF-2: Coupon Expired**

1. Customer enters a coupon code.
2. System validates the validity period.
3. Coupon has expired.
4. System rejects the coupon.
5. Customer is prompted to use another valid coupon.

---

**AF-3: Minimum Order Value Not Met**

1. Customer applies a coupon.
2. System evaluates the minimum order value.
3. Order amount is below the required threshold.
4. Coupon is rejected.
5. Checkout summary remains unchanged.

---

**AF-4: Customer Usage Limit Exceeded**

1. Customer applies a coupon.
2. System checks customer usage history.
3. Maximum usage limit has already been reached.
4. Coupon is rejected.
5. Customer continues without the coupon or applies another eligible coupon.

---

**AF-5: Coupon Not Applicable for Restaurant**

1. Customer applies a coupon.
2. System verifies restaurant eligibility.
3. Coupon is not supported for the selected restaurant.
4. Coupon is rejected.
5. Customer is notified accordingly.

---

**AF-6: Coupon Already Applied**

1. Customer attempts to apply the same coupon again.
2. System detects the duplicate request.
3. Duplicate processing is prevented.
4. Existing checkout summary is displayed.

---

**AF-7: Promotion Engine Unavailable**

1. Customer applies a coupon.
2. Promotion Engine becomes temporarily unavailable.
3. Coupon validation cannot be completed.
4. Customer receives a temporary service unavailable message.
5. Customer may retry later.

---

**AF-8: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Checkout session expires before coupon validation completes.
2. System displays a session expiration message.
3. Applied coupon information is discarded.
4. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Service Unavailable**

1. Coupon is successfully applied.
2. Audit Logging Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. System queues the audit event according to retry policy.
4. Customer continues checkout without interruption.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout session remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the coupon application later.

#### 3.5.10.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Apply Coupon at Checkout process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The validated coupon shall be successfully associated with the active checkout session.
2. The applicable discount shall be applied to the order.
3. The checkout summary shall reflect the applied coupon.
4. The order subtotal shall be updated after applying the discount.
5. Applicable taxes shall be recalculated in accordance with organizational tax rules.
6. Delivery charges shall be recalculated if affected by the coupon.
7. Applicable service fees shall be recalculated.
8. The final payable amount shall be updated.
9. The applied coupon shall remain active until removed, replaced, or the checkout session expires.
10. The checkout session shall remain active and consistent.
11. The customer shall be allowed to remove or replace the coupon before placing the order.
12. Customer shall be allowed to proceed to the Place Order step.

---

#### 3.5.10.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Apply Coupon at Checkout process, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation indicating that the coupon has been successfully applied.
2. Display the applied coupon code.
3. Display the discount amount.
4. Display the updated order subtotal.
5. Display recalculated taxes.
6. Display updated delivery charges, if applicable.
7. Display updated service fees, if applicable.
8. Display the revised final payable amount.
9. Refresh the checkout summary automatically.
10. Record the successful coupon application in the audit logs.
11. Preserve the applied coupon until modified or order placement.
12. Allow the customer to continue with the checkout process.

---

#### 3.5.10.12 Failure Response

If the Apply Coupon at Checkout process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the coupon application request.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Inform the customer if the coupon code is invalid.
4. Inform the customer if the coupon has expired.
5. Inform the customer if the minimum order value requirement is not satisfied.
6. Inform the customer if the coupon usage limit has been exceeded.
7. Inform the customer if the coupon is not applicable to the selected restaurant, products, payment method, or delivery type.
8. Preserve the existing checkout summary without applying any discount.
9. Maintain checkout session consistency.
10. Record failure details in application logs.
11. Allow the customer to retry using another eligible coupon.

---

#### 3.5.10.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Apply Coupon at Checkout functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can apply coupons.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully validated.
3. Only active and valid coupons are accepted.
4. Expired coupons are rejected.
5. Future activation coupons are rejected.
6. Customer eligibility is validated correctly.
7. Restaurant eligibility is validated correctly.
8. Product eligibility is validated correctly.
9. Payment method eligibility is validated when applicable.
10. Delivery type eligibility is validated when applicable.
11. Minimum order value requirements are enforced.
12. Maximum discount limits are enforced.
13. Customer usage limits are enforced.
14. Global coupon usage limits are enforced.
15. Applicable discount calculations are accurate.
16. Checkout summary is updated automatically after coupon application.
17. Duplicate coupon application requests are handled safely.
18. Coupon application activity is recorded in audit logs.
19. Checkout session consistency is maintained.
20. Customer can successfully proceed to order placement after applying a valid coupon.

---

#### 3.5.10.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to apply coupons.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before updating checkout information.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing requests.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections with TLS encryption.
5. Coupon validation requests shall be processed only by authorized backend services.
6. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational security policies.
7. Sensitive promotional data shall not be exposed in URLs or application logs.
8. All requests shall be validated to prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), parameter tampering, replay attacks, and other injection attacks.
9. Unauthorized coupon manipulation attempts shall be blocked and recorded.
10. Security-related events shall be logged for auditing purposes.

---

#### 3.5.10.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit events during the Apply Coupon at Checkout process:

1. Coupon application request timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID.
4. Applied Coupon Code.
5. Restaurant ID.
6. Order Amount before discount.
7. Discount Amount applied.
8. Final Payable Amount after discount.
9. Coupon validation result.
10. Promotion Engine evaluation result.
11. Customer IP Address.
12. Device Type.
13. Application Version.
14. Coupon application status (Success/Failure).
15. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
16. Audit log creation timestamp.
17. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's retention policy.

---

#### 3.5.10.16 Assumptions

The Apply Coupon at Checkout functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. Customer authentication has already been completed.
2. An active checkout session exists.
3. Checkout summary has been generated successfully.
4. Coupon Management Service is operational.
5. Promotion Engine is operational.
6. Pricing Service is operational.
7. Restaurant promotional configurations are available.
8. Network connectivity is available throughout the coupon validation process.
9. Audit Logging Service is operational.
10. All dependent backend services are operational.

---

The Apply Coupon at Checkout functionality depends on the successful availability of the following components and services:

1. Authentication Service

### 3.5.11 Apply Wallet Balance

#### 3.5.11.1 Description

The Apply Wallet Balance use case enables the customer to utilize the available wallet balance to partially or fully pay for the current order during the checkout process. The system shall retrieve the customer's available wallet balance, validate its eligibility, determine the maximum usable amount based on configured business policies, and apply the eligible wallet balance to reduce the final payable amount.

The wallet may consist of refundable balance, promotional credits, cashback rewards, loyalty credits, or other organization-supported wallet types. The applicability of each wallet component shall depend on configurable business rules, promotional policies, wallet expiry dates, transaction history, and payment restrictions.

If the available wallet balance is insufficient to cover the complete order amount, the remaining balance shall be payable using another supported payment method. If the wallet balance is sufficient, the order shall be eligible for complete payment through the wallet, subject to organizational policies.

Applying wallet balance shall automatically update the checkout summary, recalculate the remaining payable amount, refresh applicable payment options, and maintain checkout session consistency.

---

#### 3.5.11.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Wallet Management Service
- Customer Management Service
- Pricing Service
- Payment Management Service
- Promotion Engine
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.11.3 Preconditions

Before applying wallet balance during checkout, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. Checkout summary shall be successfully generated.
7. Final payable amount shall be calculated.
8. Wallet Management Service shall be operational.
9. Customer wallet shall exist.
10. Customer wallet shall be active.
11. Wallet balance information shall be available.
12. Pricing Service shall be operational.
13. Payment Management Service shall be operational.
14. Customer shall have permission to modify checkout details.
15. Required wallet configuration shall be loaded.
16. Network connectivity shall be available.
17. Audit Logging Service shall be operational.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.11.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer selects the **"Apply Wallet Balance"** option during checkout before placing the order.

---

#### 3.5.11.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Wallet ID | Customer wallet identifier | Yes | Must exist and be active |
| Available Wallet Balance | Available balance eligible for use | Yes | Retrieved from Wallet Management Service |
| Wallet Amount to Apply | Amount customer wishes to use | Conditional | Must not exceed eligible wallet balance |
| Order Amount | Current payable order amount | Yes | Must match checkout summary |
| Payment Method | Selected payment method for remaining amount | Conditional | Required if wallet balance is insufficient |
| Wallet Type | Cashback, Refund, Promotional Credit, etc. | Yes | Supported wallet type only |
| Device Information | Device details for fraud analysis | No | Valid format |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match active checkout session |

#### 3.5.11.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to apply wallet balance during checkout.
2. The wallet shall belong to the authenticated customer.
3. Only active customer wallets shall be eligible for use.
4. Wallet balance shall be retrieved in real time from the Wallet Management Service.
5. Only available wallet balance shall be considered for payment.
6. Expired wallet credits shall not be considered while calculating the available balance.
7. Promotional wallet credits shall be applied according to configured promotional policies.
8. Refund wallet credits shall be applied according to configured refund policies.
9. Cashback wallet credits shall be applied according to configured cashback policies.
10. Wallet balance shall not exceed the current payable order amount.
11. Customers shall be allowed to use partial wallet balance if supported by organizational policy.
12. Customers shall be allowed to use the entire eligible wallet balance.
13. If wallet balance fully covers the payable amount, no additional payment method shall be required.
14. If wallet balance is insufficient, the remaining amount shall be payable using another supported payment method.
15. Wallet application shall automatically update the checkout session.
16. The remaining payable amount shall be recalculated immediately after applying wallet balance.
17. Applicable payment convenience fees shall be recalculated based on the remaining payable amount.
18. Coupon discounts shall remain valid unless organizational policies specify otherwise.
19. Taxes shall be recalculated only if wallet usage affects taxable values according to applicable regulations.
20. Wallet balance shall remain reserved only until order confirmation, where applicable.
21. Wallet balance shall not be permanently deducted until successful order placement.
22. Customers shall be allowed to remove the applied wallet balance before placing the order.
23. Duplicate wallet application requests shall be processed idempotently.
24. Wallet usage restrictions configured by the organization shall be enforced.
25. Wallet usage shall comply with maximum wallet utilization limits, if configured.
26. Wallet balance shall not be transferable between customer accounts.
27. Successful wallet application events shall be recorded in audit logs.
28. Failed wallet application attempts shall also be recorded.
29. Wallet application shall not authorize payment.
30. Wallet application shall not capture payment.
31. Wallet application shall not create an order.
32. Wallet configuration changes shall become effective immediately according to deployment policies.
33. All configured business validations shall be completed before updating the checkout session.
34. Wallet balance information shall be protected according to organizational security policies.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.11.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify wallet exists.
6. Verify wallet belongs to the authenticated customer.
7. Verify wallet status is active.
8. Verify available wallet balance.
9. Verify wallet balance has not expired.
10. Verify promotional wallet credit eligibility.
11. Verify cashback wallet eligibility.
12. Verify refund wallet eligibility.
13. Verify requested wallet amount does not exceed the available eligible balance.
14. Verify requested wallet amount does not exceed the payable order amount.
15. Verify wallet utilization policy compliance.
16. Verify remaining payable amount calculation.
17. Verify convenience fee recalculation.
18. Verify coupon compatibility with wallet usage.
19. Verify tax recalculation, if applicable.
20. Verify Pricing Service availability.
21. Verify Wallet Management Service availability.
22. Verify Payment Management Service availability.
23. Verify Session Management Service availability.
24. Verify duplicate wallet application requests.
25. Verify fraud prevention policy compliance.
26. Verify audit logging service availability.
27. Verify secure transmission of wallet information.
28. Verify successful persistence of the applied wallet balance before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.11.8 Main Flow

1. Customer navigates to the **Payment** section of the checkout process.
2. System validates the customer's authentication and active checkout session.
3. System retrieves the latest checkout summary.
4. System retrieves the customer's wallet information from the Wallet Management Service.
5. System verifies that the customer's wallet is active.
6. System retrieves the available eligible wallet balance.
7. System determines the wallet credits that can be used for the current order.
8. System displays the available wallet balance to the customer.
9. Customer selects the **Apply Wallet Balance** option.
10. Customer chooses to apply the available wallet balance or enters a partial wallet amount, if supported.
11. System validates the requested wallet amount.
12. System verifies wallet eligibility according to configured business policies.
13. System verifies that the requested wallet amount does not exceed the eligible wallet balance.
14. System verifies that the requested wallet amount does not exceed the current payable order amount.
15. System determines whether additional payment is required.
16. System temporarily reserves the applicable wallet balance, where supported.
17. System recalculates the remaining payable amount.
18. System recalculates applicable payment convenience fees.
19. System recalculates applicable taxes, if required.
20. System updates the checkout session with the applied wallet balance.
21. System updates the selected payment method, if the remaining balance requires an additional payment.
22. System refreshes the checkout summary.
23. System updates the payment breakdown displayed to the customer.
24. System displays the remaining wallet balance after application.
25. System records the wallet application event in the audit logs.
26. System displays a confirmation indicating successful wallet application.
27. Customer reviews the updated payable amount.
28. Customer confirms the payment details.
29. Customer proceeds to the **Place Order** step.
30. System maintains checkout session consistency throughout the process.

---

#### 3.5.11.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Insufficient Wallet Balance**

1. Customer attempts to apply wallet balance.
2. System determines that the available wallet balance is less than the requested amount.
3. System displays the available eligible balance.
4. Customer applies a lower wallet amount or selects another payment method.
5. Checkout summary is refreshed.

---

**AF-2: Wallet Balance Fully Covers Order Amount**

1. Customer applies the available wallet balance.
2. System determines that the wallet balance fully covers the payable amount.
3. Remaining payable amount becomes zero.
4. Additional payment method is not required.
5. Customer proceeds to order placement.

---

**AF-3: Wallet Expired**

1. Customer attempts to apply wallet balance.
2. System detects that the wallet credits have expired.
3. Expired wallet credits are excluded.
4. Customer is informed accordingly.
5. Customer continues with another payment option.

---

**AF-4: Wallet Temporarily Unavailable**

1. Wallet Management Service is temporarily unavailable.
2. System cannot retrieve the wallet balance.
3. Customer receives a temporary service unavailable message.
4. Customer proceeds using another supported payment method or retries later.

---

**AF-5: Wallet Usage Restricted**

1. Customer attempts to apply wallet balance.
2. System determines that wallet usage is restricted for the current order.
3. Wallet application is rejected.
4. Customer is informed about the applicable restriction.
5. Checkout continues without wallet usage.

---

**AF-6: Coupon and Wallet Policy Conflict**

1. Customer applies wallet balance after applying a coupon.
2. System evaluates organizational promotional policies.
3. Wallet usage affects coupon eligibility.
4. System recalculates the checkout summary.
5. Customer reviews the updated payable amount before proceeding.

---

**AF-7: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Checkout session expires before wallet application completes.
2. System displays a session expiration message.
3. Reserved wallet balance, if any, is released.
4. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-8: Wallet Reservation Failure**

1. Wallet validation succeeds.
2. Temporary wallet reservation fails due to a concurrent transaction.
3. System informs the customer.
4. Wallet application is cancelled.
5. Customer retries the operation.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Service Unavailable**

1. Wallet balance is successfully applied.
2. Audit Logging Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. System queues the audit event according to retry policy.
4. Customer continues checkout without interruption.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout session remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the wallet application later.

#### 3.5.11.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Apply Wallet Balance process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The eligible wallet balance shall be successfully associated with the active checkout session.
2. The applicable wallet amount shall be reserved temporarily until order confirmation, where supported.
3. The remaining payable amount shall be updated.
4. Applicable payment convenience fees shall be recalculated.
5. Applicable taxes shall be recalculated if required by organizational policies.
6. The checkout summary shall display the applied wallet amount.
7. The updated wallet balance shall be displayed to the customer.
8. The selected payment method shall be updated if additional payment is required.
9. The checkout session shall remain active and consistent.
10. The customer shall be allowed to remove or modify the applied wallet balance before placing the order.
11. Wallet funds shall not be permanently deducted until successful order placement.
12. Customer shall be allowed to proceed to the Place Order step.

---

#### 3.5.11.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Apply Wallet Balance process, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation indicating that the wallet balance has been successfully applied.
2. Display the applied wallet amount.
3. Display the remaining wallet balance.
4. Display the updated order subtotal.
5. Display recalculated convenience fees, if applicable.
6. Display recalculated taxes, if applicable.
7. Display the revised final payable amount.
8. Refresh the checkout summary automatically.
9. Persist the wallet application information in the active checkout session.
10. Record the successful wallet application in the audit logs.
11. Preserve the applied wallet balance until modified or order placement.
12. Allow the customer to continue to the Place Order process.

---

#### 3.5.11.12 Failure Response

If the Apply Wallet Balance process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the wallet application request.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Inform the customer if the wallet balance is insufficient.
4. Inform the customer if wallet credits have expired.
5. Inform the customer if wallet usage is restricted for the current order.
6. Inform the customer if the Wallet Management Service is temporarily unavailable.
7. Preserve the existing checkout summary without applying wallet balance.
8. Maintain checkout session consistency.
9. Release any temporary wallet reservation created during processing.
10. Record failure details in application logs.
11. Allow the customer to retry using wallet balance or another supported payment method.

---

#### 3.5.11.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Apply Wallet Balance functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can apply wallet balance.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully validated.
3. Wallet ownership is verified correctly.
4. Only active wallets are accepted.
5. Expired wallet credits are excluded automatically.
6. Promotional wallet policies are enforced.
7. Cashback wallet policies are enforced.
8. Refund wallet policies are enforced.
9. Wallet amount cannot exceed the eligible balance.
10. Wallet amount cannot exceed the payable order amount.
11. Remaining payable amount is calculated accurately.
12. Convenience fees are recalculated correctly.
13. Taxes are recalculated where applicable.
14. Checkout summary is updated automatically.
15. Wallet funds are not permanently deducted before order confirmation.
16. Duplicate wallet application requests are handled safely.
17. Wallet application activity is recorded in audit logs.
18. Checkout session consistency is maintained.
19. All configured wallet utilization policies are enforced.
20. Customer can successfully proceed to order placement after applying wallet balance.

---

#### 3.5.11.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to apply wallet balance.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before updating checkout information.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing requests.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections with TLS encryption.
5. Wallet balances shall be retrieved only through authorized backend services.
6. Wallet balances and transaction information shall never be exposed to unauthorized users.
7. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational security policies.
8. Sensitive wallet information shall not be exposed in URLs, client-side logs, or application logs.
9. All requests shall be validated to prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), parameter tampering, replay attacks, and other injection attacks.
10. Unauthorized wallet usage attempts shall be blocked and recorded for security auditing.

---

#### 3.5.11.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit events during the Apply Wallet Balance process:

1. Wallet application request timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID.
4. Wallet ID.
5. Wallet Type.
6. Available wallet balance before application.
7. Applied wallet amount.
8. Remaining wallet balance after application.
9. Remaining payable amount.
10. Wallet validation result.
11. Customer IP Address.
12. Device Type.
13. Application Version.
14. Wallet application status (Success/Failure).
15. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
16. Audit log creation timestamp.
17. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's retention policy.

---

#### 3.5.11.16 Assumptions

The Apply Wallet Balance functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. Customer authentication has already been completed.
2. An active checkout session exists.
3. Checkout summary has been generated successfully.
4. Customer wallet exists and is active.
5. Wallet Management Service is operational.
6. Pricing Service is operational.
7. Payment Management Service is operational.
8. Network connectivity is available throughout the wallet application process.
9. Audit Logging Service is operational.
10. All dependent backend services are operational.

The Apply Wallet Balance functionality depends on the successful availability of the following components and services:

1. Authentication Service
2. Checkout Service
3. Wallet Management Service

### 3.5.12 Add Delivery Instructions

#### 3.5.12.1 Description

The Add Delivery Instructions use case enables the customer to provide additional delivery-related instructions to assist the delivery partner in completing the order accurately and efficiently. These instructions are associated with the current checkout session and are made available to authorized stakeholders involved in order fulfillment.

Delivery instructions may include apartment or building access details, floor number, landmark information, gate access codes, preferred drop-off location, contact preferences, handling instructions, pet warnings, or other delivery-specific notes. The system shall validate the instructions against configured content policies, maximum length restrictions, prohibited content rules, and security guidelines.

The delivery instructions shall be stored securely and linked to the order after successful order placement. Authorized delivery partners, restaurant personnel (where applicable), and customer support representatives shall be able to access the instructions according to their role-based permissions.

Customers shall be allowed to add, edit, or remove delivery instructions any time before the order is confirmed. Adding delivery instructions shall not affect pricing, taxes, delivery charges, payment processing, or order eligibility.

---

#### 3.5.12.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Delivery Management Service
- Customer Management Service
- Order Management Service
- Content Validation Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.12.3 Preconditions

Before adding delivery instructions, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. A valid delivery address shall already be selected.
7. Delivery type shall already be selected.
8. Checkout summary shall be available.
9. Delivery Management Service shall be operational.
10. Order Management Service shall be operational.
11. Content Validation Service shall be operational.
12. Session Management Service shall be operational.
13. Customer shall have permission to modify checkout details.
14. Required configuration settings shall be loaded.
15. Network connectivity shall be available.
16. Audit Logging Service shall be operational.
17. Database Management System shall be operational.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.12.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer selects the **"Add Delivery Instructions"** option during the checkout process before placing the order.

---

#### 3.5.12.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Delivery Instruction | Delivery notes entered by the customer | Yes | Must satisfy configured content policies |
| Instruction Category | Apartment, Landmark, Contact, Gate Access, Drop-off, etc. | No | Configured values only |
| Preferred Contact Method | Call, SMS, Doorbell, No Call, etc. | No | Supported values only |
| Building/Floor Details | Apartment or office location details | No | Maximum configured length |
| Landmark | Nearby landmark for easy identification | No | Maximum configured length |
| Special Handling Note | Fragile, Leave at Door, Pet Warning, etc. | No | Maximum configured length |
| Device Information | Device details for audit purposes | No | Valid format |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match active checkout session |

#### 3.5.12.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to add delivery instructions during checkout.
2. Delivery instructions shall be associated only with the authenticated customer's active checkout session.
3. Customers shall be allowed to provide delivery instructions before placing the order.
4. Delivery instructions shall apply only to the current order unless explicitly saved as a default preference.
5. Customers shall be allowed to edit delivery instructions until the order is confirmed.
6. Customers shall be allowed to remove delivery instructions before order confirmation.
7. Delivery instructions shall not exceed the configured maximum character limit.
8. Empty or whitespace-only instructions shall not be accepted.
9. Delivery instructions shall be validated using the Content Validation Service.
10. Offensive, abusive, or prohibited language shall not be accepted.
11. Personally sensitive information prohibited by organizational policy shall not be accepted.
12. Delivery instructions shall support Unicode characters according to platform configuration.
13. HTML, JavaScript, SQL commands, and executable content shall not be accepted.
14. Special characters shall be validated according to configured security policies.
15. Delivery instructions shall not affect product pricing.
16. Delivery instructions shall not affect taxes.
17. Delivery instructions shall not affect delivery charges.
18. Delivery instructions shall not affect coupon eligibility.
19. Delivery instructions shall not affect wallet balance calculations.
20. Delivery instructions shall not affect payment processing.
21. Delivery instructions shall be securely stored with the checkout session.
22. Delivery instructions shall become part of the order after successful order placement.
23. Only authorized delivery personnel shall be allowed to view delivery instructions.
24. Restaurant personnel shall only access delivery instructions where operationally required.
25. Customer Support representatives shall access delivery instructions only according to role-based permissions.
26. Duplicate submission requests shall be processed idempotently.
27. Successful delivery instruction updates shall be recorded in audit logs.
28. Failed delivery instruction submissions shall also be recorded.
29. Delivery instruction configuration shall be dynamically configurable.
30. Delivery instructions shall comply with organizational privacy policies.
31. Delivery instructions shall remain editable until order confirmation.
32. Delivery instructions shall not create an order.
33. Delivery instructions shall not authorize payment.
34. All configured business validations shall be completed before updating the checkout session.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.12.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify delivery instruction is provided.
6. Verify delivery instruction is not empty.
7. Verify maximum character length.
8. Verify prohibited language restrictions.
9. Verify offensive content restrictions.
10. Verify restricted content policies.
11. Verify HTML injection prevention.
12. Verify JavaScript injection prevention.
13. Verify SQL injection prevention.
14. Verify supported Unicode characters.
15. Verify special character policy compliance.
16. Verify instruction category validity.
17. Verify preferred contact method validity.
18. Verify building or floor details length.
19. Verify landmark length.
20. Verify special handling note length.
21. Verify Content Validation Service availability.
22. Verify Delivery Management Service availability.
23. Verify Session Management Service availability.
24. Verify Order Management Service availability.
25. Verify duplicate submission requests.
26. Verify audit logging service availability.
27. Verify secure transmission of delivery instructions.
28. Verify successful persistence of delivery instructions before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.12.8 Main Flow

1. Customer navigates to the checkout process.
2. System validates the customer's authentication.
3. System validates the active checkout session.
4. System retrieves the selected delivery address.
5. System retrieves the current checkout summary.
6. System displays the **Add Delivery Instructions** option.
7. Customer selects the option to add delivery instructions.
8. System displays the delivery instruction input form.
9. Customer enters delivery instructions.
10. Customer optionally selects an instruction category.
11. Customer optionally provides building or floor details.
12. Customer optionally provides landmark information.
13. Customer optionally specifies the preferred contact method.
14. Customer optionally enters special handling notes.
15. Customer reviews the entered delivery instructions.
16. Customer submits the delivery instructions.
17. System validates all mandatory fields.
18. System validates the instruction content against configured content policies.
19. System checks for prohibited language and restricted content.
20. System validates the maximum character length.
21. System sanitizes the input to prevent malicious content.
22. System updates the active checkout session.
23. System securely stores the delivery instructions.
24. System associates the delivery instructions with the current checkout session.
25. System records the successful update in the audit logs.
26. System refreshes the checkout summary.
27. System displays the saved delivery instructions.
28. Customer reviews the updated checkout information.
29. Customer proceeds with the remaining checkout steps.
30. System maintains checkout session consistency until order placement.

---

#### 3.5.12.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Empty Delivery Instructions**

1. Customer submits an empty instruction.
2. System detects that no valid content has been entered.
3. System displays an appropriate validation message.
4. Customer enters valid delivery instructions.
5. Processing continues.

---

**AF-2: Maximum Character Limit Exceeded**

1. Customer enters instructions exceeding the configured character limit.
2. System rejects the submission.
3. System displays the permitted character limit.
4. Customer shortens the instruction.
5. Submission is retried.

---

**AF-3: Prohibited Language Detected**

1. Customer submits delivery instructions.
2. System detects prohibited or offensive language.
3. Submission is rejected.
4. Customer is informed to modify the content.
5. Customer submits updated instructions.

---

**AF-4: Invalid Special Characters**

1. Customer enters unsupported or restricted characters.
2. System validates the input.
3. Invalid content is rejected.
4. Customer updates the delivery instructions.
5. Validation is performed again.

---

**AF-5: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Customer attempts to save delivery instructions.
2. System detects that the checkout session has expired.
3. Customer is informed accordingly.
4. Unsaved changes are discarded.
5. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-6: Customer Removes Delivery Instructions**

1. Customer selects the remove option.
2. System deletes the delivery instructions from the checkout session.
3. Checkout summary is refreshed.
4. Audit logs are updated.
5. Customer continues checkout.

---

**AF-7: Customer Updates Existing Instructions**

1. Customer edits previously saved delivery instructions.
2. System validates the updated content.
3. Existing instructions are replaced with the latest version.
4. Checkout session is updated.
5. Customer reviews the updated instructions.

---

**AF-8: Content Validation Service Unavailable**

1. Customer submits delivery instructions.
2. Content Validation Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. System displays a temporary service unavailable message.
4. Delivery instructions are not saved.
5. Customer retries later.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Service Unavailable**

1. Delivery instructions are successfully saved.
2. Audit Logging Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. System queues the audit event for retry according to organizational policy.
4. Checkout continues without interruption.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout session remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the operation later.

#### 3.5.12.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Add Delivery Instructions process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. Delivery instructions shall be successfully associated with the active checkout session.
2. The latest delivery instructions shall be securely stored.
3. The selected delivery address shall remain unchanged.
4. Checkout session consistency shall be maintained.
5. Delivery instructions shall be available for subsequent checkout steps.
6. The customer shall be allowed to edit or remove the delivery instructions until the order is confirmed.
7. Delivery instructions shall become part of the order after successful order placement.
8. Authorized delivery personnel shall be able to access the instructions during order fulfillment.
9. The checkout summary shall reflect the presence of delivery instructions.
10. Successful updates shall be recorded in the audit logs.
11. Existing payment selections shall remain unchanged.
12. Customer shall be allowed to continue to the next checkout step.

---

#### 3.5.12.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the Add Delivery Instructions process, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation indicating that the delivery instructions have been saved successfully.
2. Display the latest delivery instructions in the checkout summary.
3. Display the selected instruction category, if provided.
4. Preserve all previously entered checkout information.
5. Maintain the active checkout session.
6. Store the delivery instructions securely.
7. Associate the instructions with the current checkout session.
8. Record the successful operation in the audit logs.
9. Refresh the checkout information automatically.
10. Allow the customer to edit or remove the delivery instructions before order confirmation.
11. Allow the customer to continue with the remaining checkout process.
12. Preserve the delivery instructions for order creation.

---

#### 3.5.12.12 Failure Response

If the Add Delivery Instructions process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the invalid submission.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Inform the customer if prohibited content is detected.
4. Inform the customer if the maximum character limit is exceeded.
5. Inform the customer if unsupported characters are detected.
6. Inform the customer if the checkout session has expired.
7. Preserve previously saved delivery instructions, if any.
8. Maintain checkout session consistency.
9. Prevent invalid delivery instructions from being stored.
10. Record failure details in application logs.
11. Allow the customer to correct the input and retry the operation.

---

#### 3.5.12.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Add Delivery Instructions functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can add delivery instructions.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully validated.
3. Delivery instructions are securely associated with the active checkout session.
4. Empty delivery instructions are rejected.
5. Maximum character limits are enforced.
6. Offensive or prohibited language is rejected.
7. HTML and executable content are blocked.
8. SQL injection attempts are prevented.
9. JavaScript injection attempts are prevented.
10. Supported Unicode characters are accepted.
11. Delivery instructions can be edited before order confirmation.
12. Delivery instructions can be removed before order confirmation.
13. Checkout summary is updated automatically.
14. Delivery instructions do not affect pricing or payment calculations.
15. Delivery instructions are available during order fulfillment.
16. Role-based access to delivery instructions is enforced.
17. Successful updates are recorded in audit logs.
18. Duplicate requests are handled safely.
19. Checkout session consistency is maintained.
20. Customer can continue with the checkout process after successfully saving delivery instructions.

---

#### 3.5.12.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to add delivery instructions.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before updating checkout information.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing requests.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections with TLS encryption.
5. Delivery instructions shall be sanitized before storage.
6. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational privacy policies.
7. Sensitive customer information shall not be exposed in logs or URLs.
8. Role-based access control shall restrict visibility of delivery instructions.
9. Input validation shall prevent SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), parameter tampering, replay attacks, and other injection attacks.

### 3.5.13 Validate Checkout Information

#### 3.5.13.1 Description

The Validate Checkout Information use case performs a comprehensive verification of all checkout information before the customer is allowed to place the order. The objective is to ensure that all mandatory checkout data is complete, accurate, consistent, and compliant with business policies to prevent invalid, incomplete, duplicate, or fraudulent orders.

The system shall validate customer authentication, shopping cart contents, restaurant availability, menu item availability, inventory status, pricing accuracy, taxes, delivery charges, delivery address, delivery type, scheduled delivery details (if applicable), applied coupons, wallet balance, selected payment method, delivery instructions, and all other checkout information.

The validation process shall execute in real time by retrieving the latest information from dependent services to ensure that the checkout reflects the current business state. If any validation fails, the system shall display the appropriate validation message, refresh the affected checkout information if necessary, and prevent order placement until all validation errors are resolved.

Successful validation does not create an order or authorize payment. It only confirms that the checkout information is complete and eligible for order placement.

---

#### 3.5.13.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Customer Management Service
- Cart Management Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Menu Management Service
- Inventory Management Service
- Pricing Service
- Coupon Management Service
- Wallet Management Service
- Payment Management Service
- Delivery Management Service
- Order Management Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Session Management Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.13.3 Preconditions

Before validating checkout information, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. A valid restaurant shall be selected.
7. Menu items shall exist in the shopping cart.
8. Delivery address shall already be selected.
9. Delivery type shall already be selected.
10. Checkout summary shall be generated successfully.
11. Pricing Service shall be operational.
12. Inventory Management Service shall be operational.
13. Payment Management Service shall be operational.
14. Coupon and Wallet services shall be available if applicable.
15. Delivery Management Service shall be operational.
16. Session Management Service shall be operational.
17. Audit Logging Service shall be operational.
18. All dependent backend services shall be available.

---

#### 3.5.13.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer clicks **"Continue"**, **"Review Order"**, or **"Place Order"**, requiring the system to validate all checkout information before proceeding to order placement.

---

#### 3.5.13.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Cart ID | Customer shopping cart identifier | Yes | Must exist |
| Restaurant ID | Selected restaurant identifier | Yes | Must exist and be active |
| Delivery Address ID | Selected delivery address | Yes | Must exist |
| Delivery Type | Immediate or Scheduled Delivery | Yes | Supported values only |
| Scheduled Delivery Time | Requested delivery slot | Conditional | Required for scheduled delivery |
| Applied Coupon Code | Selected coupon | Conditional | Must remain valid |
| Applied Wallet Amount | Wallet amount applied | Conditional | Must remain eligible |
| Payment Method | Selected payment method | Yes | Supported method only |
| Delivery Instructions | Delivery notes | No | Must satisfy content policies |
| Final Payable Amount | Calculated payable amount | Yes | Must match latest pricing |
| Device Information | Device details for fraud detection | No | Valid format |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match active session |

#### 3.5.13.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to validate checkout information.
2. Checkout validation shall be performed immediately before order placement.
3. The checkout session shall belong to the authenticated customer.
4. The checkout session shall be active and unexpired.
5. The shopping cart shall contain at least one valid menu item.
6. Every menu item shall belong to the selected restaurant.
7. The selected restaurant shall be active and accepting orders.
8. All menu items shall be available at the time of validation.
9. Inventory availability shall be verified in real time.
10. Menu prices shall be revalidated against the latest pricing configuration.
11. Taxes shall be recalculated using the latest tax configuration.
12. Delivery charges shall be recalculated using the latest delivery rules.
13. Platform fees, if applicable, shall be recalculated.
14. Applied coupons shall be revalidated before proceeding.
15. Expired or invalid coupons shall be automatically rejected.
16. Applied wallet balance shall be revalidated.
17. Wallet eligibility rules shall be enforced.
18. The final payable amount shall be recalculated before validation succeeds.
19. The selected payment method shall remain active and supported.
20. Scheduled delivery slots shall be revalidated before confirmation.
21. Delivery address shall remain serviceable.
22. Delivery instructions shall comply with configured content policies.
23. Duplicate validation requests shall be processed idempotently.
24. Validation shall not create an order.
25. Validation shall not authorize payment.
26. Validation shall not deduct wallet balance.
27. Validation shall not reserve inventory permanently.
28. Validation results shall be stored in the active checkout session.
29. Successful validations shall be recorded in audit logs.
30. Failed validations shall also be recorded.
31. Validation failures shall clearly identify the affected checkout component.
32. Checkout information shall be refreshed whenever stale information is detected.
33. All dependent services shall successfully complete validation before checkout is considered valid.
34. Business rule configurations shall be dynamically configurable without application code changes.
35. The system shall maintain complete transactional consistency throughout the validation process.

---

#### 3.5.13.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify shopping cart is not empty.
6. Verify restaurant availability.
7. Verify restaurant is accepting orders.
8. Verify menu item availability.
9. Verify inventory availability.
10. Verify latest menu pricing.
11. Verify tax calculation.
12. Verify delivery charge calculation.
13. Verify platform fee calculation, if applicable.
14. Verify coupon validity.
15. Verify coupon eligibility.
16. Verify wallet eligibility.
17. Verify wallet balance availability.
18. Verify payment method validity.
19. Verify delivery address validity.
20. Verify delivery serviceability.
21. Verify scheduled delivery slot availability, if applicable.
22. Verify delivery instructions comply with content policies.
23. Verify final payable amount accuracy.
24. Verify Pricing Service availability.
25. Verify Inventory Management Service availability.
26. Verify Payment Management Service availability.
27. Verify duplicate validation requests.
28. Verify successful persistence of validation results before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.13.8 Main Flow

1. Customer reviews the checkout summary.
2. Customer clicks **Continue**, **Review Order**, or **Place Order**.
3. System receives the checkout validation request.
4. System validates the customer's authentication status.
5. System validates the active checkout session.
6. System retrieves the latest checkout information.
7. System retrieves the latest shopping cart details.
8. System verifies that the shopping cart is not empty.
9. System verifies that the selected restaurant is active and accepting orders.
10. System verifies the availability of all menu items.
11. System checks the latest inventory availability for each menu item.
12. System validates the selected delivery address.
13. System verifies delivery serviceability for the selected address.
14. System validates the selected delivery type.
15. System validates the scheduled delivery slot, if applicable.
16. System recalculates product prices using the latest pricing rules.
17. System recalculates applicable taxes.
18. System recalculates delivery charges.
19. System recalculates platform fees, if applicable.
20. System revalidates the applied coupon, if any.
21. System revalidates the applied wallet balance, if any.
22. System validates the selected payment method.
23. System validates the delivery instructions, if provided.
24. System recalculates the final payable amount.
25. System performs fraud and risk validation according to configured policies.
26. System compares the latest calculated values with the checkout session.
27. System updates the checkout session if refreshed values are generated.
28. System records the successful validation in the audit logs.
29. System displays that the checkout information has been successfully validated.
30. Customer proceeds to the **Place Order** process.

---

#### 3.5.13.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Shopping Cart is Empty**

1. System detects that the shopping cart contains no items.
2. Checkout validation fails.
3. Customer is informed that the cart is empty.
4. Customer is redirected to the shopping cart.

---

**AF-2: Restaurant is Closed**

1. System validates the restaurant status.
2. Restaurant is currently unavailable or closed.
3. Validation fails.
4. Customer is informed accordingly.
5. Customer may select another restaurant.

---

**AF-3: Menu Item Unavailable**

1. System verifies menu item availability.
2. One or more items are unavailable.
3. Validation fails.
4. Customer is notified about unavailable items.
5. Customer updates the shopping cart.

---

**AF-4: Inventory Changed**

1. System detects insufficient inventory.
2. Validation fails.
3. Customer is informed of the affected products.
4. Customer modifies the order.
5. Validation can be performed again.

---

**AF-5: Coupon Expired**

1. Applied coupon is revalidated.
2. Coupon is no longer valid.
3. Coupon is removed.
4. Checkout summary is recalculated.
5. Customer reviews the updated payable amount.

---

**AF-6: Wallet Balance Changed**

1. Wallet balance is revalidated.
2. Eligible wallet balance has changed.
3. Applied wallet amount is adjusted or removed.
4. Checkout summary is refreshed.
5. Customer continues with the updated payment information.

---

**AF-7: Delivery Area No Longer Serviceable**

1. System validates delivery coverage.
2. Selected address is no longer serviceable.
3. Validation fails.
4. Customer selects another delivery address.
5. Validation is retried.

---

**AF-8: Payment Method Unavailable**

1. System validates the selected payment method.
2. Payment method is unavailable.
3. Customer is prompted to select another supported payment method.
4. Checkout session is updated.
5. Validation continues.

---

**AF-9: Checkout Session Expired**

1. System detects that the checkout session has expired.
2. Validation is terminated.
3. Customer is informed accordingly.
4. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected system error occurs during validation.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout session remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the validation later.

#### 3.5.13.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Validate Checkout Information process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. All checkout information shall be successfully validated.
2. The latest pricing information shall be reflected in the checkout session.
3. Product availability shall be confirmed.
4. Inventory availability shall be confirmed.
5. Delivery serviceability shall be verified.
6. Applied coupon eligibility shall be confirmed or updated.
7. Applied wallet eligibility shall be confirmed or updated.
8. The selected payment method shall remain valid.
9. The final payable amount shall be recalculated and updated.
10. Validation results shall be stored in the active checkout session.
11. Successful validation shall be recorded in the audit logs.
12. Customer shall be allowed to proceed to the Place Order process.

---

#### 3.5.13.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of checkout validation, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation indicating that checkout information has been successfully validated.
2. Display the latest checkout summary.
3. Display the verified product list.
4. Display the verified pricing details.
5. Display updated taxes and delivery charges, if applicable.
6. Display the validated coupon information, if applicable.
7. Display the validated wallet information, if applicable.
8. Display the final payable amount.
9. Persist the validation results in the checkout session.
10. Record the successful validation in the audit logs.
11. Refresh the checkout page, if required.
12. Allow the customer to continue to the Place Order process.

---

#### 3.5.13.12 Failure Response

If checkout validation cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the validation request.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Highlight the checkout section that requires customer attention.
4. Inform the customer if product availability has changed.
5. Inform the customer if pricing has changed.
6. Inform the customer if inventory is unavailable.
7. Inform the customer if the coupon has expired or become invalid.
8. Inform the customer if the wallet balance has changed.
9. Inform the customer if the delivery address is no longer serviceable.
10. Preserve the checkout session wherever possible.
11. Record failure details in application logs.
12. Allow the customer to correct the identified issues and retry validation.

---

The Validate Checkout Information functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can perform checkout validation.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully verified.
3. Shopping cart availability is validated.
4. Restaurant availability is verified.
5. Product availability is verified.
6. Inventory availability is verified.
7. Latest pricing is successfully recalculated.
8. Taxes are recalculated accurately.
9. Delivery charges are recalculated accurately.
10. Coupon eligibility is successfully verified.
11. Wallet eligibility is successfully verified.
12. Payment method validity is confirmed.
13. Delivery address serviceability is verified.
14. Scheduled delivery slot is validated where applicable.
15. Final payable amount is accurately calculated.
16. Successful validations are recorded in audit logs.
17. Duplicate validation requests are handled safely.
18. Checkout session consistency is maintained.
19. Validation does not create an order or authorize payment.
20. Customer can proceed to the Place Order process after successful validation.

---

#### 3.5.13.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to validate checkout information.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before processing validation requests.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections with TLS encryption.
5. Sensitive checkout data shall be transmitted securely.
