### 3.5.14 Calculate Final Payable Amount

#### 3.5.14.1 Description

The Calculate Final Payable Amount use case determines the final amount payable by the customer before order placement. The calculation shall consider all applicable pricing components, including product subtotal, taxes, delivery charges, packaging charges, platform fees, surge pricing, discounts, promotional offers, coupon deductions, wallet balance, reward points (if applicable), and any additional configurable charges.

The system shall perform all calculations in real time using the latest pricing configuration to ensure pricing accuracy and consistency. Every pricing component shall be individually validated before calculating the final payable amount. The calculation process shall also verify applicable business rules, promotional policies, tax regulations, and payment eligibility.

The calculated final payable amount shall be displayed transparently to the customer with a detailed price breakdown. If any pricing component changes during checkout, the system shall automatically recalculate the payable amount and update the checkout summary before allowing the customer to proceed to order placement.

This use case shall not authorize payment, reserve inventory, or create an order. It is solely responsible for determining the final payable amount for the current checkout session.

---

#### 3.5.14.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Pricing Service
- Tax Calculation Service
- Coupon Management Service
- Wallet Management Service
- Payment Management Service
- Promotion Engine
- Restaurant Management Service
- Delivery Management Service
- Session Management Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.14.3 Preconditions

Before calculating the final payable amount, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. A valid restaurant shall be selected.
7. Product prices shall be available.
8. Delivery address shall already be selected.
9. Delivery type shall already be selected.
10. Checkout summary shall be generated.
11. Pricing Service shall be operational.
12. Tax Calculation Service shall be operational.
13. Coupon Management Service shall be available if a coupon is applied.
14. Wallet Management Service shall be available if wallet balance is applied.
15. Delivery Management Service shall be operational.
16. Session Management Service shall be operational.
17. Audit Logging Service shall be operational.
18. All dependent backend services shall be available.

---

#### 3.5.14.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated whenever the customer opens the checkout summary or when any pricing-related information changes, including cart contents, delivery address, delivery type, coupon, wallet balance, or payment method.

---

#### 3.5.14.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Cart ID | Shopping cart identifier | Yes | Must exist |
| Restaurant ID | Selected restaurant identifier | Yes | Must be active |
| Product Subtotal | Total product cost before additional charges | Yes | Must match current cart |
| Delivery Charge | Delivery fee | Conditional | Calculated using delivery rules |
| Packaging Charge | Restaurant packaging fee | Conditional | Configured pricing rules |
| Platform Fee | Platform service fee | Conditional | Configured pricing rules |
| Tax Amount | Applicable tax amount | Yes | Calculated using latest tax configuration |
| Coupon Discount | Discount applied using coupon | Conditional | Must remain eligible |
| Wallet Amount | Wallet balance applied | Conditional | Must remain eligible |
| Reward Points Amount | Reward point redemption amount | Conditional | Must remain eligible |
| Final Payable Amount | Final calculated payable amount | Yes | System calculated |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match active checkout session |

#### 3.5.14.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to calculate the final payable amount.
2. The calculation shall be performed using the customer's active checkout session.
3. The shopping cart shall contain at least one valid menu item.
4. Product prices shall be retrieved from the latest pricing configuration.
5. Product subtotal shall equal the sum of all eligible cart items.
6. Delivery charges shall be calculated using the configured delivery pricing rules.
7. Packaging charges shall be calculated based on restaurant-specific configurations.
8. Platform fees shall be calculated according to the organization's pricing policy.
9. Applicable taxes shall be calculated using the latest tax configuration.
10. Surge pricing shall be applied only when the configured conditions are satisfied.
11. Promotional discounts shall be applied before calculating the final payable amount.
12. Coupon discounts shall be applied only after successful coupon validation.
13. Only one coupon shall be applicable unless multiple coupon usage is explicitly supported.
14. Wallet balance shall be deducted only after successful wallet validation.
15. Reward points shall be deducted only if redemption policies are satisfied.
16. The payable amount shall never become negative.
17. The minimum payable amount shall comply with organizational payment policies.
18. All monetary values shall be rounded according to configured financial rounding rules.
19. Currency shall remain consistent throughout the checkout session.
20. Every pricing component shall be recalculated whenever checkout information changes.
21. Recalculation shall occur automatically after coupon application or removal.
22. Recalculation shall occur automatically after wallet balance application or removal.
23. Recalculation shall occur automatically after delivery address changes.
24. Recalculation shall occur automatically after delivery type changes.
25. Recalculation shall occur automatically after shopping cart modifications.
26. Duplicate calculation requests shall be processed idempotently.
27. Successful calculations shall be recorded in audit logs.
28. Failed calculations shall also be recorded.
29. Calculation shall not authorize payment.
30. Calculation shall not reserve inventory.
31. Calculation shall not create an order.
32. All dependent pricing services shall complete successfully before the calculation is finalized.
33. Pricing configurations shall be dynamically configurable without application code changes.
34. Customers shall be presented with a transparent price breakdown for every pricing component.
35. The system shall maintain complete pricing consistency throughout the checkout process.

---

#### 3.5.14.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify shopping cart is not empty.
6. Verify product pricing availability.
7. Verify product subtotal accuracy.
8. Verify delivery charge calculation.
9. Verify packaging charge calculation.
10. Verify platform fee calculation.
11. Verify applicable tax calculation.
12. Verify surge pricing eligibility.
13. Verify promotional discount eligibility.
14. Verify coupon validity.
15. Verify coupon discount calculation.
16. Verify wallet eligibility.
17. Verify wallet deduction amount.
18. Verify reward point eligibility.
19. Verify reward point deduction amount.
20. Verify final payable amount is greater than or equal to zero.
21. Verify supported currency configuration.
22. Verify Pricing Service availability.
23. Verify Tax Calculation Service availability.
24. Verify Coupon Management Service availability.
25. Verify Wallet Management Service availability.
26. Verify duplicate calculation requests.
27. Verify financial rounding rules are correctly applied.
28. Verify successful persistence of the calculated payable amount before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.14.8 Main Flow

1. Customer opens the checkout summary.
2. System validates the customer's authentication.
3. System validates the active checkout session.
4. System retrieves the latest shopping cart details.
5. System retrieves the latest product prices.
6. System calculates the product subtotal.
7. System retrieves the applicable delivery pricing rules.
8. System calculates the delivery charges.
9. System retrieves the restaurant packaging charge configuration.
10. System calculates the packaging charges.
11. System retrieves the applicable platform fee configuration.
12. System calculates the platform fee.
13. System retrieves the latest tax configuration.
14. System calculates the applicable taxes.
15. System evaluates surge pricing conditions, if applicable.
16. System applies surge pricing according to configured policies.
17. System retrieves applicable promotional offers.
18. System validates and applies promotional discounts.
19. System validates the applied coupon, if any.
20. System applies the eligible coupon discount.
21. System validates the applied wallet balance, if any.
22. System deducts the eligible wallet amount.
23. System validates redeemed reward points, if applicable.
24. System deducts the eligible reward point value.
25. System calculates the final payable amount.
26. System applies configured financial rounding rules.
27. System updates the checkout summary with the latest pricing breakdown.
28. System records the successful calculation in the audit logs.
29. System displays the updated final payable amount to the customer.
30. Customer reviews the pricing details and proceeds with checkout.

---

#### 3.5.14.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Product Price Updated**

1. System detects that one or more product prices have changed.
2. Product subtotal is recalculated.
3. Checkout summary is refreshed.
4. Customer is informed about the updated pricing.
5. Customer reviews the revised payable amount.

---

**AF-2: Delivery Charge Changed**

1. System recalculates the delivery charges.
2. Updated delivery fee differs from the previous value.
3. Checkout summary is refreshed.
4. Customer is notified of the revised delivery charge.
5. Calculation continues.

---

**AF-3: Coupon Becomes Invalid**

1. System validates the applied coupon.
2. Coupon is no longer eligible.
3. Coupon discount is removed.
4. Final payable amount is recalculated.
5. Customer reviews the updated pricing.

---

**AF-4: Wallet Balance Changed**

1. System revalidates the wallet balance.
2. Eligible wallet amount has changed.
3. Wallet deduction is updated.
4. Final payable amount is recalculated.
5. Customer reviews the revised payment summary.

---

**AF-5: Reward Point Validation Failed**

1. Reward point redemption is validated.
2. Reward points are no longer eligible.
3. Reward point deduction is removed.
4. Checkout summary is updated.
5. Customer continues with the revised payable amount.

---

**AF-6: Tax Configuration Updated**

1. System retrieves the latest tax configuration.
2. Tax amount changes.
3. Final payable amount is recalculated.
4. Updated taxes are displayed.
5. Customer reviews the revised checkout summary.

---

**AF-7: Checkout Session Expired**

1. System detects that the checkout session has expired.
2. Calculation is terminated.
3. Customer is informed accordingly.
4. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-8: Pricing Service Unavailable**

1. Pricing Service becomes unavailable.
2. Final payable amount cannot be calculated.
3. Customer receives a temporary service unavailable message.
4. Checkout calculation is not completed.
5. Customer retries later.

---

**AF-9: Duplicate Calculation Request**

1. System receives multiple identical calculation requests.
2. Duplicate requests are identified.
3. Only one calculation request is processed.
4. Duplicate requests return the same calculation result.
5. Checkout session remains consistent.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout summary remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the calculation later.

#### 3.5.14.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Calculate Final Payable Amount process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. The final payable amount shall be successfully calculated.
2. The latest product subtotal shall be available in the checkout summary.
3. Delivery charges shall be updated according to the latest pricing rules.
4. Packaging charges shall be reflected in the checkout summary, where applicable.
5. Platform fees shall be updated, where applicable.
6. Applicable taxes shall be calculated and displayed.
7. Eligible promotional discounts shall be applied.
8. Valid coupon discounts shall be reflected in the checkout summary.
9. Eligible wallet deductions shall be reflected in the payable amount.
10. Eligible reward point deductions shall be reflected, where applicable.

12. Successful calculation shall be recorded in the audit logs.
13. Checkout session consistency shall be maintained.
14. Customer shall be allowed to proceed to the next checkout step.

2. Display the delivery charge.

4. Display the platform fee, if applicable.
5. Display the applicable tax amount.

7. Display coupon discounts, if applicable.

10. Display the final payable amount.
11. Display a complete pricing breakdown for transparency.
12. Persist the calculated pricing information in the checkout session.
13. Record the successful calculation in the audit logs.
14. Allow the customer to continue to the next checkout step.

---

#### 3.5.14.12 Failure Response

If the Calculate Final Payable Amount process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the calculation request.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Inform the customer if product pricing has changed.
4. Inform the customer if delivery charges have changed.
5. Inform the customer if taxes could not be calculated.
6. Inform the customer if the applied coupon is no longer valid.
7. Inform the customer if the wallet balance has changed.
8. Inform the customer if reward point redemption is no longer eligible.
9. Preserve the previously calculated checkout summary wherever possible.
10. Record failure details in application logs.
11. Allow the customer to retry the calculation after resolving the identified issue.

---

#### 3.5.14.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Calculate Final Payable Amount functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can calculate the final payable amount.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully verified.
3. Product subtotal is calculated accurately.
4. Delivery charges are calculated according to configured rules.
5. Packaging charges are calculated correctly.
6. Platform fees are calculated correctly.
7. Applicable taxes are calculated accurately.
8. Promotional discounts are applied correctly.
9. Coupon discounts are applied only after successful validation.
10. Wallet deductions are applied only after successful validation.
11. Reward point deductions are applied only after successful validation.
12. Final payable amount is accurately calculated.
13. Financial rounding rules are correctly applied.
14. Transparent pricing breakdown is displayed.
15. Duplicate calculation requests are handled safely.
16. Successful calculations are recorded in audit logs.
17. Checkout session consistency is maintained.
18. Calculation does not authorize payment.
19. Calculation does not create an order.
20. Customer can proceed with checkout after successful calculation.

#### 3.5.14.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to calculate the final payable amount.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before performing calculations.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections with TLS encryption.
5. Sensitive pricing information shall be transmitted securely.
6. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational privacy policies.
7. Sensitive pricing information shall not be exposed in URLs, client-side logs, or error messages.
8. Role-based access control shall restrict pricing calculation operations to authorized users and backend services.
9. All requests shall be protected against SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), replay attacks, parameter tampering, and other injection attacks.
10. Unauthorized calculation attempts shall be blocked and recorded for security auditing.

### 3.5.15 Display Estimated Delivery Time

#### 3.5.15.1 Description

The Display Estimated Delivery Time use case enables the system to calculate and present the estimated delivery time (EDT) for the customer's order before order placement. The estimated delivery time shall be calculated using real-time operational data, including restaurant preparation time, current kitchen workload, delivery partner availability, traffic conditions, weather conditions (where supported), delivery distance, scheduled delivery preferences, and other configurable business rules.

The system shall continuously recalculate the estimated delivery time whenever any checkout information affecting delivery changes, such as delivery address, delivery type, restaurant selection, shopping cart contents, or scheduled delivery preferences.

The estimated delivery time shall be displayed as a customer-friendly delivery window (for example, **25–35 minutes**) or a scheduled delivery slot, depending on the selected delivery type. The displayed estimate shall improve customer transparency and assist in making informed purchasing decisions.

Displaying the estimated delivery time shall not reserve a delivery partner, reserve inventory, authorize payment, or create an order. It only provides the customer with the latest estimated delivery commitment based on the current checkout information.

---

#### 3.5.15.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Delivery Management Service
- Restaurant Management Service
- Kitchen Management Service
- Driver Allocation Service
- Traffic Intelligence Service
- Route Optimization Service
- Location Service
- Scheduling Service
- Pricing Service
- Notification Service
- Session Management Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.15.3 Preconditions

Before displaying the estimated delivery time, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one menu item.
6. A valid restaurant shall be selected.
7. Delivery address shall already be selected.
8. Delivery type shall already be be selected.
9. Restaurant shall be accepting orders.
10. Checkout summary shall be available.
11. Delivery Management Service shall be operational.
12. Restaurant Management Service shall be operational.
13. Route Optimization Service shall be operational.
14. Location Service shall be operational.
15. Scheduling Service shall be operational.
16. Session Management Service shall be operational.
17. Audit Logging Service shall be operational.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.15.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated whenever the customer opens the checkout summary or whenever any checkout information changes that may affect the estimated delivery time, including restaurant selection, delivery address, delivery type, scheduled delivery preference, or shopping cart contents.

---

#### 3.5.15.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Restaurant ID | Selected restaurant identifier | Yes | Must exist and be active |
| Delivery Address ID | Selected delivery location | Yes | Must exist and be serviceable |
| Delivery Type | Standard, Express, Scheduled, etc. | Yes | Supported values only |
| Scheduled Delivery Time | Preferred delivery slot | Conditional | Required for scheduled delivery |
| Shopping Cart Details | Current cart items | Yes | Must contain valid menu items |
| Estimated Preparation Time | Restaurant preparation estimate | Yes | Retrieved from Restaurant Management Service |
| Estimated Travel Distance | Distance between restaurant and delivery location | Yes | Calculated by Route Optimization Service |
| Current Traffic Information | Live traffic conditions | Conditional | Retrieved from Traffic Intelligence Service |
| Delivery Partner Availability | Nearby delivery partner availability | Yes | Retrieved from Driver Allocation Service |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match active checkout session |

#### 3.5.15.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to view the estimated delivery time.
2. Estimated delivery time shall be calculated using the customer's active checkout session.
3. The selected restaurant shall be active and accepting orders.
4. Delivery shall be available for the selected delivery address.
5. Restaurant preparation time shall be included in the calculation.
6. Current kitchen workload shall be considered while estimating preparation time.
7. Delivery partner availability shall be considered during estimation.
8. Live traffic conditions shall be considered where supported.
9. Route optimization shall be used to estimate travel duration.
10. Delivery distance shall influence the estimated delivery time.
11. Weather conditions may be considered if supported by the organization.
12. Scheduled delivery orders shall display the selected delivery slot instead of an immediate delivery estimate.
13. Express delivery estimates shall follow express delivery policies.
14. Delivery estimates shall be recalculated whenever checkout information changes.
15. Delivery estimates shall be recalculated whenever the delivery address changes.
16. Delivery estimates shall be recalculated whenever the restaurant changes.
17. Delivery estimates shall be recalculated whenever the delivery type changes.
18. Delivery estimates shall be recalculated whenever shopping cart contents affect preparation time.
19. Estimated delivery time shall be displayed as a delivery window whenever configured.
20. Estimated delivery time shall not be guaranteed unless explicitly stated by organizational policy.
21. Customers shall be informed that actual delivery time may vary due to operational conditions.
22. Delivery estimation shall not reserve a delivery partner.
23. Delivery estimation shall not reserve inventory.
24. Delivery estimation shall not authorize payment.
25. Delivery estimation shall not create an order.
26. Duplicate estimation requests shall be processed idempotently.
27. Successful delivery estimations shall be recorded in audit logs.
28. Failed estimation attempts shall also be recorded.
29. Estimation algorithms shall use the latest operational data available.
30. Business configuration changes shall become effective without application code changes.
31. All dependent services shall complete successfully before displaying the estimate.
32. Cached delivery estimates shall be refreshed according to configured cache policies.
33. Estimated delivery time shall remain consistent across all customer interfaces.
34. System-generated estimates shall follow configured Service Level Agreement (SLA) policies.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.15.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify restaurant availability.
6. Verify restaurant is accepting orders.
7. Verify delivery address validity.
8. Verify delivery address serviceability.
9. Verify delivery type validity.
10. Verify scheduled delivery slot, if applicable.
11. Verify shopping cart contains valid items.
12. Verify restaurant preparation time availability.
13. Verify kitchen workload information availability.
14. Verify delivery partner availability.
15. Verify route optimization result availability.
16. Verify travel distance calculation.
17. Verify traffic information availability, where applicable.
18. Verify estimated travel duration.
19. Verify delivery estimation algorithm execution.
20. Verify Delivery Management Service availability.
21. Verify Restaurant Management Service availability.
22. Verify Driver Allocation Service availability.
23. Verify Route Optimization Service availability.
24. Verify Location Service availability.
25. Verify Scheduling Service availability.
26. Verify duplicate estimation requests.
27. Verify audit logging service availability.
28. Verify successful persistence of the estimated delivery time before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.15.8 Main Flow

1. Customer opens the checkout summary.
2. System validates the customer's authentication.
3. System validates the active checkout session.
4. System retrieves the latest checkout information.
5. System retrieves the selected restaurant information.
6. System verifies that the restaurant is accepting orders.
7. System retrieves the selected delivery address.
8. System validates the delivery serviceability for the selected address.
9. System retrieves the selected delivery type.
10. System retrieves the restaurant's estimated preparation time.
11. System retrieves the current kitchen workload.
12. System retrieves the availability of nearby delivery partners.
13. System retrieves the estimated delivery distance.
14. System retrieves the optimized delivery route.
15. System retrieves current traffic information, where supported.
16. System retrieves scheduled delivery information, if applicable.
17. System calculates the estimated preparation duration.
18. System calculates the estimated travel duration.
19. System applies configured business rules affecting delivery estimation.
20. System determines the overall estimated delivery time.
21. System converts the estimate into a customer-friendly delivery window.
22. System updates the checkout session with the latest estimated delivery time.
23. System records the successful estimation in the audit logs.
24. System refreshes the checkout summary.
25. System displays the estimated delivery time.
26. Customer reviews the displayed delivery estimate.
27. Customer continues reviewing the checkout information.
28. Customer proceeds with the remaining checkout steps.
29. System maintains checkout session consistency.
30. System continuously refreshes the estimate whenever qualifying checkout information changes.

---

#### 3.5.15.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Restaurant Preparation Time Updated**

1. System detects that the restaurant preparation time has changed.
2. Estimated delivery time is recalculated.
3. Checkout summary is refreshed.
4. Customer is informed about the updated estimate.
5. Checkout continues.

---

**AF-2: Delivery Address Changed**

2. System recalculates the delivery distance.
3. Travel duration is recalculated.
4. Estimated delivery time is updated.
5. Checkout summary is refreshed.

---

**AF-3: Delivery Partner Unavailable**

1. System detects limited delivery partner availability.
2. Estimated waiting time increases.
3. Estimated delivery time is recalculated.
4. Customer is informed of the updated delivery estimate.
5. Checkout continues.

---

**AF-4: Heavy Traffic Detected**

1. System retrieves live traffic information.
2. Heavy traffic affects the delivery route.
3. Travel duration increases.
4. Estimated delivery time is recalculated.
5. Updated estimate is displayed.

---

**AF-5: Scheduled Delivery Selected**

1. Customer selects scheduled delivery.
2. System validates the selected delivery slot.
3. Scheduled delivery window is displayed instead of an immediate delivery estimate.
4. Customer reviews the selected delivery slot.
5. Checkout continues.

---

**AF-6: Restaurant Stops Accepting Orders**

1. Restaurant status changes during checkout.
2. System detects that the restaurant is unavailable.
3. Estimated delivery time cannot be calculated.
4. Customer is informed accordingly.
5. Checkout is prevented until another restaurant is selected.

---

**AF-7: Route Optimization Service Unavailable**

3. Customer receives a temporary service unavailable message.
4. Delivery estimate is not displayed.
5. Customer may retry later.

---

**AF-8: Checkout Session Expired**

1. System detects that the checkout session has expired.
2. Estimated delivery calculation is terminated.
3. Customer is informed accordingly.
4. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-9: Duplicate Estimation Request**

1. System receives multiple identical estimation requests.
2. Duplicate requests are detected.
3. Only one request is processed.
4. Remaining requests return the existing estimate.
5. Checkout session remains consistent.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Previously displayed estimate remains unchanged, if available.
5. Customer may retry later.

### 3.5.16 Accept Terms and Conditions

#### 3.5.16.1 Description

The Accept Terms and Conditions use case enables the customer to review and explicitly accept the organization's Terms and Conditions, Privacy Policy, Refund Policy, Cancellation Policy, and other applicable legal agreements before placing an order. The system shall require explicit customer consent before proceeding to the final order placement process.

The system shall present the latest published version of the applicable legal documents and record the customer's acceptance along with the document version, acceptance timestamp, device information, and other audit details. If the organization updates any legal policy, the customer shall be required to accept the latest version before placing subsequent orders.

Acceptance of the Terms and Conditions shall constitute the customer's acknowledgement that they understand and agree to the organization's policies governing ordering, payments, cancellations, refunds, delivery, privacy, data processing, and other applicable legal obligations.

Failure to accept the required legal agreements shall prevent the customer from proceeding to order placement. Accepting the Terms and Conditions shall not authorize payment, reserve inventory, or create an order. It only satisfies the mandatory legal consent requirement for checkout completion.

---

#### 3.5.16.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Legal Document Management Service
- Consent Management Service
- Customer Management Service
- Session Management Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Notification Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.16.3 Preconditions

Before accepting the Terms and Conditions, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one product.
6. Checkout summary shall be available.
7. Latest Terms and Conditions document shall be published.
8. Latest Privacy Policy shall be available.
9. Latest Refund Policy shall be available, where applicable.
10. Latest Cancellation Policy shall be available, where applicable.
11. Legal Document Management Service shall be operational.
12. Consent Management Service shall be operational.
13. Session Management Service shall be operational.
14. Customer shall have permission to continue checkout.
15. Required legal document versions shall be loaded.
16. Network connectivity shall be available.
17. Audit Logging Service shall be operational.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.16.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer reaches the final checkout stage where acceptance of the Terms and Conditions is mandatory before placing the order.

---

#### 3.5.16.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Terms and Conditions Version | Current published version | Yes | Must be the latest active version |
| Privacy Policy Version | Current published version | Yes | Must be the latest active version |
| Refund Policy Version | Current published version | Conditional | Required if applicable |
| Cancellation Policy Version | Current published version | Conditional | Required if applicable |
| Acceptance Checkbox | Customer consent confirmation | Yes | Must be explicitly selected |
| Acceptance Timestamp | Date and time of customer consent | Yes | System generated |
| Device Information | Device details for audit purposes | No | Valid format |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match the active checkout session |

#### 3.5.16.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to accept the Terms and Conditions.
2. Acceptance of the Terms and Conditions shall be mandatory before placing an order.
3. Customers shall explicitly provide consent by selecting the acceptance checkbox.
4. Pre-selected or implied consent shall not be considered valid.
5. The latest published version of the Terms and Conditions shall always be presented.
6. The latest published Privacy Policy shall always be presented.
7. The latest published Refund Policy shall be presented where applicable.
8. The latest published Cancellation Policy shall be presented where applicable.
9. Customers shall be allowed to access and review all legal documents before providing consent.
10. Acceptance shall apply only to the current published versions of the legal documents.
11. If any legal document version changes before order placement, the customer shall be required to accept the updated version.
12. Acceptance shall be associated with the authenticated customer's account.
13. Acceptance shall be associated with the active checkout session.
14. Acceptance timestamp shall be generated by the system.
15. Legal document version numbers shall be recorded.
16. Customer device information shall be captured for audit purposes where supported.
17. Customer IP address shall be recorded according to organizational audit policies.
18. Consent records shall be immutable after successful submission.
19. Acceptance records shall be retained according to legal and regulatory retention requirements.
20. Acceptance shall not authorize payment.
21. Acceptance shall not reserve inventory.
22. Acceptance shall not create an order.
23. Acceptance shall not deduct wallet balance.
24. Duplicate acceptance requests shall be processed idempotently.
25. Customers shall be allowed to reopen and review legal documents before providing consent.
26. Consent Management Service shall validate every acceptance request.
27. Successful consent submissions shall be recorded in audit logs.
28. Failed consent submissions shall also be recorded.
29. Organizational legal document configurations shall be dynamically configurable.
30. Legal documents shall be displayed according to the customer's selected language, where supported.
31. Acceptance records shall be securely stored.
32. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) associated with consent shall be protected.
33. All dependent services shall successfully complete processing before consent is finalized.
34. Acceptance shall remain valid only for the applicable document versions.
35. The system shall maintain complete consistency across all dependent services.

---

#### 3.5.16.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify the latest Terms and Conditions version is available.
6. Verify the latest Privacy Policy version is available.
7. Verify the latest Refund Policy version, where applicable.
8. Verify the latest Cancellation Policy version, where applicable.
9. Verify legal document version consistency.
10. Verify acceptance checkbox has been selected.
11. Verify explicit customer consent has been provided.
12. Verify consent request has not expired.
13. Verify customer has permission to continue checkout.
14. Verify Consent Management Service availability.
15. Verify Legal Document Management Service availability.
16. Verify Session Management Service availability.
17. Verify audit logging service availability.
18. Verify acceptance timestamp generation.
19. Verify legal document version capture.
20. Verify customer identifier consistency.
21. Verify checkout version consistency.
22. Verify duplicate consent requests.
23. Verify secure transmission of consent information.
24. Verify consent record persistence.
25. Verify customer device information format, where applicable.
26. Verify IP address capture, where applicable.
27. Verify organizational consent policy compliance.
28. Verify successful persistence of the consent record before completing the operation.

#### 3.5.16.8 Main Flow

1. Customer reaches the final stage of the checkout process.
2. System validates the customer's authentication.
3. System validates the active checkout session.
4. System determines whether legal consent is mandatory.
5. System retrieves the latest published Terms and Conditions.
6. System retrieves the latest published Privacy Policy.
7. System retrieves the latest Refund Policy, where applicable.
8. System retrieves the latest Cancellation Policy, where applicable.
9. System displays links to all applicable legal documents.
10. Customer reviews one or more legal documents, if desired.
11. Customer returns to the checkout page.
12. System displays the consent statement.
13. Customer selects the **Accept Terms and Conditions** checkbox.
14. Customer confirms their acceptance.
15. System validates that explicit consent has been provided.
16. System verifies that the displayed document versions are still the latest published versions.
17. System generates the acceptance timestamp.
18. System captures the accepted legal document versions.
19. System captures customer device information, where supported.
20. System captures the customer IP address according to organizational policy.
21. System creates a consent record.
22. System securely stores the consent record.
23. System associates the consent record with the active checkout session.
24. System associates the consent record with the authenticated customer account.
25. System records the successful consent event in the audit logs.
26. System updates the checkout status to indicate that legal consent has been completed.
27. System refreshes the checkout summary.
28. System displays confirmation that the Terms and Conditions have been accepted.
29. Customer reviews the updated checkout information.
30. Customer proceeds to the **Place Order** process.

---

#### 3.5.16.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Customer Does Not Accept Terms and Conditions**

1. Customer reaches the final checkout stage.
2. Customer does not select the acceptance checkbox.
3. System prevents further checkout progression.
4. Customer is informed that acceptance is mandatory.
5. Customer reviews the legal documents and provides consent.

---

**AF-2: Legal Document Version Updated**

1. Customer is reviewing the legal documents.
2. A newer version of one or more legal documents is published.
3. System detects the version mismatch.
4. Updated legal documents are displayed.
5. Customer reviews and accepts the latest version.

---

**AF-3: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Customer attempts to submit consent.
2. System detects that the checkout session has expired.
3. Consent submission is cancelled.
4. Customer is informed accordingly.
5. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-4: Consent Record Persistence Failure**

1. Customer successfully provides consent.
2. System cannot save the consent record.
3. Checkout progression is stopped.
4. Customer receives a temporary error message.
5. Customer retries the operation.

---

**AF-5: Legal Document Service Unavailable**

1. System attempts to retrieve legal documents.
2. Legal Document Management Service is unavailable.
3. Legal documents cannot be displayed.
4. Customer is informed accordingly.
5. Customer retries later.

---

**AF-6: Consent Management Service Unavailable**

1. Customer submits consent.
2. Consent Management Service is unavailable.
3. Consent cannot be processed.
4. Checkout progression is blocked.
5. Customer retries later.

---

**AF-7: Duplicate Consent Submission**

1. Customer submits multiple consent requests.
2. System detects duplicate requests.
3. Only one consent record is created.
4. Duplicate requests receive the existing response.
5. Checkout continues normally.

---

**AF-8: Invalid Legal Document Version**

1. System validates the accepted document versions.
2. One or more document versions are invalid.
3. Customer is required to reload the latest legal documents.
4. Consent process restarts.
5. Checkout continues after successful acceptance.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Service Unavailable**

1. Consent is successfully recorded.
2. Audit Logging Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. Audit event is queued according to retry policies.
4. Customer continues checkout without interruption.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Customer receives a generic error message.
4. Checkout session remains unchanged.
5. Customer may retry the consent process later.

#### 3.5.16.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Accept Terms and Conditions process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer consent shall be successfully recorded.
2. The accepted Terms and Conditions version shall be stored.
3. The accepted Privacy Policy version shall be stored.
4. The accepted Refund Policy version shall be stored, where applicable.
5. The accepted Cancellation Policy version shall be stored, where applicable.
6. The consent record shall be associated with the authenticated customer.
7. The consent record shall be associated with the active checkout session.
8. Consent timestamp shall be permanently recorded.
9. Customer device information shall be stored where supported.
10. Customer IP address shall be stored according to organizational policies.
11. Successful consent shall be recorded in the audit logs.
12. Checkout session shall indicate that mandatory legal consent has been completed.
13. Checkout session consistency shall be maintained.
14. Customer shall be allowed to proceed to the Place Order process.

---

#### 3.5.16.11 Success Response

Upon successful completion of the consent process, the system shall:

1. Display confirmation that the Terms and Conditions have been successfully accepted.
2. Display confirmation that the customer's consent has been recorded.
3. Display the accepted legal document versions where applicable.
4. Persist the consent record in the Consent Management Service.
5. Associate the consent record with the active checkout session.
6. Associate the consent record with the authenticated customer account.
7. Update the checkout status to indicate successful legal consent.
8. Refresh the checkout summary automatically.
9. Record the successful consent event in the audit logs.
10. Maintain checkout session consistency.
11. Preserve the consent record for legal compliance.
12. Allow the customer to continue to the Place Order process.

---

#### 3.5.16.12 Failure Response

If the Accept Terms and Conditions process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the consent submission.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Inform the customer if mandatory consent has not been provided.
4. Inform the customer if the displayed legal document version has changed.
5. Inform the customer if the Legal Document Management Service is unavailable.
6. Inform the customer if the Consent Management Service is unavailable.
7. Prevent progression to the Place Order process.
8. Preserve the checkout session wherever possible.
9. Record failure details in application logs.
10. Maintain checkout session consistency.
11. Allow the customer to retry after resolving the identified issue.

---

#### 3.5.16.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Accept Terms and Conditions functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can submit legal consent.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully verified.
3. The latest Terms and Conditions are displayed.
4. The latest Privacy Policy is displayed.
5. Refund Policy is displayed where applicable.
6. Cancellation Policy is displayed where applicable.
7. Explicit customer consent is mandatory.
8. Pre-selected consent is not accepted.
9. Latest legal document versions are recorded.
10. Consent timestamp is generated by the system.
11. Customer consent is associated with the authenticated account.
12. Customer consent is associated with the active checkout session.
13. Duplicate consent submissions are handled safely.
14. Successful consent records are stored securely.
15. Successful consent events are recorded in audit logs.
16. Consent records remain immutable after submission.
17. Checkout session consistency is maintained.
18. Consent does not authorize payment.
19. Consent does not create an order.
20. Customer can proceed to the Place Order process only after successful consent.

---

#### 3.5.16.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to submit legal consent.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before processing consent requests.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing.
4. All communication shall occur over secure HTTPS connections with TLS encryption.
5. Consent records shall be securely stored using organizational security standards.
6. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational privacy policies.
7. Legal consent information shall not be exposed in URLs, logs, or client-side error messages.
8. Role-based access control shall restrict consent management operations to authorized users and backend services.
9. All requests shall be protected against SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), replay attacks, parameter tampering, and other injection attacks.
10. Unauthorized consent submission attempts shall be blocked and recorded for security auditing.

---

#### 3.5.16.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit events during the Accept Terms and Conditions process:

1. Consent submission timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID.
4. Terms and Conditions version.
5. Privacy Policy version.
6. Refund Policy version, where applicable.
7. Cancellation Policy version, where applicable.
8. Consent status (Accepted/Rejected).
9. Customer IP Address.
10. Device Type.
11. Browser or Application Version.
12. Consent record identifier.
13. Audit log creation timestamp.
14. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
15. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's legal retention policy.

---

#### 3.5.16.16 Assumptions

The Accept Terms and Conditions functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. Customer authentication has already been completed.
2. An active checkout session exists.
3. Mandatory checkout information has already been completed.
4. Latest legal documents have been published.
5. Legal Document Management Service is operational.
6. Consent Management Service is operational.
7. Session Management Service is operational.
8. Network connectivity is available throughout the consent process.
9. Audit Logging Service is operational.
10. All dependent backend services are operational.

---

The Accept Terms and Conditions functionality depends on the successful availability of the following components and services:

1. Authentication Service
2. Checkout Service
3. Legal Document Management Service
4. Consent Management Service
5. Customer Management Service
6. Session Management Service
7. Audit Logging Service

### 3.5.17 Confirm Order

#### 3.5.17.1 Description

The Confirm Order use case enables the customer to perform the final confirmation of the order after successfully completing all mandatory checkout activities. Before confirming the order, the system shall ensure that all checkout information has been validated, pricing is finalized, legal consent has been recorded, payment requirements have been satisfied, and the order is eligible for submission.

Upon customer confirmation, the system shall perform the final pre-order verification, lock the checkout session to prevent concurrent modifications, and initiate the order placement workflow. The confirmation process serves as the customer's final acknowledgement that all order details—including selected items, quantities, delivery address, delivery type, estimated delivery time, pricing, taxes, discounts, wallet deductions, payment method, and delivery instructions—are accurate and accepted.

Once the customer confirms the order, no further checkout modifications shall be permitted unless the order placement process fails and organizational policies allow recovery. Successful confirmation shall initiate the subsequent Place Order workflow but shall not independently create the order until the order creation process completes successfully.

---

#### 3.5.17.2 Actors

**Primary Actor**

- Customer

**Supporting Actors**

- Authentication Service
- Checkout Service
- Order Management Service
- Payment Management Service
- Pricing Service
- Inventory Management Service
- Delivery Management Service
- Session Management Service
- Notification Service
- Fraud Detection Service
- Audit Logging Service
- Database Management System

---

#### 3.5.17.3 Preconditions

Before confirming the order, the following conditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer shall be authenticated.
2. Customer account shall be active.
3. A valid checkout session shall exist.
4. Checkout session shall not have expired.
5. Shopping cart shall contain at least one valid menu item.
6. Checkout validation shall have completed successfully.
7. Final payable amount shall have been calculated.
8. Estimated delivery time shall have been calculated.
9. Delivery address shall have been selected.
10. Delivery type shall have been selected.
11. Payment method shall have been selected.
12. Mandatory legal consent shall have been recorded.
13. All pricing information shall be current.
14. Checkout session shall not be locked by another transaction.
15. Order Management Service shall be operational.
16. Session Management Service shall be operational.
17. Audit Logging Service shall be operational.
18. All dependent backend services shall be operational.

---

#### 3.5.17.4 Trigger

The use case is initiated when the customer clicks the **"Confirm Order"** button on the final checkout confirmation screen.

---

#### 3.5.17.5 Input Fields

| Field Name | Description | Mandatory | Validation |
|------------|-------------|-----------|------------|
| Checkout Session ID | Active checkout session identifier | Yes | Must be valid |
| Customer ID | Authenticated customer identifier | Yes | Must match logged-in customer |
| Checkout Confirmation Flag | Customer confirmation indicator | Yes | Must be explicitly confirmed |
| Final Payable Amount | Final calculated payable amount | Yes | Must match the latest checkout calculation |
| Payment Method | Selected payment method | Yes | Must be valid and active |
| Delivery Address ID | Selected delivery address | Yes | Must exist and be serviceable |
| Delivery Type | Standard, Express, Scheduled, etc. | Yes | Supported values only |
| Estimated Delivery Time | Latest calculated delivery estimate | Yes | Must be current |
| Device Information | Device details for audit purposes | No | Valid format |
| Checkout Version | Current checkout version | Yes | Must match the active checkout session |

#### 3.5.17.6 Business Rules

1. Only authenticated customers shall be allowed to confirm an order.
2. Order confirmation shall be permitted only after successful checkout validation.
3. Customer shall explicitly confirm the order before the order placement workflow begins.
4. The final payable amount shall remain unchanged during confirmation.
5. Any modification to the checkout after price calculation shall require recalculation before confirmation.
6. A valid delivery address shall be mandatory.
7. A valid delivery type shall be mandatory.
8. A valid payment method shall be mandatory.
9. Mandatory legal consent shall be completed before confirmation.
10. The checkout session shall be locked immediately after successful confirmation to prevent concurrent modifications.
11. Once confirmed, checkout information shall become read-only.
12. The system shall verify that menu items are still available before proceeding.
13. The system shall verify restaurant operational status before proceeding.
14. The system shall verify serviceability of the delivery address.
15. Fraud detection checks shall be completed before initiating order placement.
16. Duplicate confirmation requests shall be handled idempotently.
17. Expired checkout sessions shall not be allowed to confirm orders.
18. Confirmation shall not directly create an order record.
19. Confirmation shall initiate the Place Order workflow.
20. Confirmation shall not deduct inventory independently.
21. Confirmation shall not capture payment independently unless required by the selected payment flow.
22. Successful confirmation shall be associated with the active checkout session.
23. Successful confirmation shall be associated with the authenticated customer.
24. Confirmation timestamp shall be generated by the system.
25. Confirmation events shall be recorded in audit logs.
26. Failed confirmation attempts shall also be recorded.
27. System-generated values shall not be modifiable by the customer.
28. All validations shall complete successfully before confirmation.
29. Backend services shall maintain transactional consistency throughout the confirmation process.
30. The checkout session shall remain recoverable if confirmation fails before order placement.
31. Organizational checkout policies shall be configurable.
32. Sensitive checkout information shall be protected throughout the confirmation process.
33. Confirmation shall comply with organizational security policies.
34. Confirmation shall comply with applicable legal and regulatory requirements.
35. The system shall maintain complete data integrity throughout the confirmation process.

---

#### 3.5.17.7 Validations

1. Verify customer authentication.
2. Verify customer account status.
3. Verify checkout session validity.
4. Verify checkout session ownership.
5. Verify checkout session has not expired.
6. Verify customer has explicitly confirmed the order.
7. Verify checkout validation has been completed successfully.
8. Verify delivery address availability and serviceability.
9. Verify delivery type validity.
10. Verify payment method validity.
11. Verify final payable amount consistency.
12. Verify estimated delivery time is current.
13. Verify mandatory legal consent exists.
14. Verify restaurant is currently accepting orders.
15. Verify selected menu items are available.
16. Verify pricing information has not changed.
17. Verify coupon validity, where applicable.
18. Verify wallet balance calculation, where applicable.
19. Verify fraud detection service availability.
20. Verify Order Management Service availability.
21. Verify Session Management Service availability.
22. Verify Audit Logging Service availability.
23. Verify checkout version consistency.
24. Verify duplicate confirmation requests.
25. Verify checkout session lock status.
26. Verify secure transmission of confirmation request.
27. Verify audit event generation.
28. Verify all mandatory backend validations complete successfully.

#### 3.5.17.8 Main Flow

1. Customer reviews the final checkout summary.
2. Customer verifies all order details.
3. Customer clicks the **Confirm Order** button.
4. System receives the confirmation request.
5. System validates the customer's authentication.
6. System validates the active checkout session.
7. System verifies checkout session ownership.
8. System verifies that the checkout session has not expired.
9. System verifies that mandatory legal consent has been completed.
10. System verifies the selected delivery address.
11. System verifies the selected delivery type.
12. System verifies the selected payment method.
13. System verifies the final payable amount.
14. System verifies that restaurant pricing has not changed.
15. System verifies that all selected menu items remain available.
16. System verifies that the restaurant is currently accepting orders.
17. System verifies delivery serviceability.
18. System performs fraud detection checks.
19. System validates all checkout information one final time.
20. System locks the checkout session to prevent further modifications.
21. System generates the confirmation timestamp.
22. System records the confirmation event.
23. System associates the confirmation with the authenticated customer.
24. System associates the confirmation with the active checkout session.
25. System records the confirmation details in the audit logs.
26. System updates the checkout status as **Order Confirmed**.
27. System prepares the checkout session for order placement.
28. System displays an order confirmation progress indicator.
29. System initiates the **Place Order** workflow.
30. Customer waits while the order placement process begins.

---

#### 3.5.17.9 Alternate Flows

**AF-1: Customer Cancels Confirmation**

1. Customer reviews the checkout summary.
2. Customer decides not to confirm the order.
3. Customer exits the confirmation page.
4. Checkout session remains editable.
5. Customer may resume checkout later.

---

**AF-2: Checkout Session Expired**

1. Customer submits the confirmation request.
2. System detects that the checkout session has expired.
3. Confirmation request is rejected.
4. Customer is informed that the session has expired.
5. Customer restarts the checkout process.

---

**AF-3: Restaurant Closed**

1. System validates restaurant availability.
2. Restaurant is no longer accepting orders.
3. Confirmation process is stopped.
4. Customer receives an appropriate notification.
5. Customer may choose another restaurant.

---

**AF-4: Menu Item Unavailable**

1. System validates menu availability.
2. One or more selected items are unavailable.
3. Confirmation process is cancelled.
4. Customer is prompted to update the cart.
5. Checkout resumes after modifications.

---

**AF-5: Price Changed Before Confirmation**

1. System detects updated pricing.
2. Checkout totals are recalculated.
3. Customer is shown the revised pricing.
4. Customer reviews the updated order.
5. Customer confirms again.

---

**AF-6: Fraud Detection Failed**

1. Fraud detection identifies a suspicious transaction.
2. Confirmation process is suspended.
3. Order placement is blocked.
4. Security event is recorded.
5. Customer receives an appropriate notification.

---

**AF-7: Duplicate Confirmation Request**

1. Multiple confirmation requests are received.
2. System detects duplicate submissions.
3. Only the first request is processed.
4. Remaining requests receive the existing response.
5. Checkout consistency is maintained.

---

**AF-8: Checkout Session Already Locked**

1. Confirmation request is received.
2. Checkout session is already locked.
3. System rejects additional confirmation attempts.
4. Customer is informed that processing is already in progress.
5. Duplicate processing is prevented.

---

**AF-9: Audit Logging Service Unavailable**

1. Confirmation completes successfully.
2. Audit Logging Service is temporarily unavailable.
3. Audit event is queued according to retry policies.
4. Confirmation continues without interruption.

---

**AF-10: Internal System Error**

1. An unexpected application error occurs.
2. System records the exception.
3. Checkout session remains recoverable.
4. Customer receives a generic error message.
5. Customer may retry the confirmation process.

#### 3.5.17.10 Postconditions

Upon successful completion of the Confirm Order process, the following postconditions shall be satisfied:

1. Customer's order confirmation shall be successfully recorded.
2. The checkout session shall be locked against further modifications.
3. Checkout status shall be updated as **Order Confirmed**.
4. Confirmation timestamp shall be permanently stored.
5. Confirmation record shall be associated with the authenticated customer.
6. Confirmation record shall be associated with the active checkout session.
7. Final checkout information shall be preserved without modification.
8. Customer confirmation event shall be recorded in the audit logs.
9. Checkout session shall be marked ready for the Place Order workflow.
10. Fraud detection verification status shall be recorded.
11. Confirmation response shall be available for subsequent order processing.
12. Customer shall be eligible to proceed to the Place Order process.
13. System consistency shall be maintained across all dependent services.
14. No order shall be created until the Place Order workflow completes successfully.

---

#### 3.5.17.11 Success Response

Upon successful confirmation, the system shall:

1. Display a confirmation message indicating that the order has been confirmed.
2. Display the latest confirmed checkout summary.
3. Display that the checkout session is being processed.
4. Lock the checkout session against further edits.
5. Record the confirmation timestamp.
6. Associate the confirmation with the authenticated customer.
7. Associate the confirmation with the active checkout session.
8. Persist the confirmation record.
9. Record the successful confirmation event in the audit logs.
10. Update the checkout status to **Order Confirmed**.
11. Initiate the Place Order workflow.
12. Maintain transactional consistency throughout the process.

---

#### 3.5.17.12 Failure Response

If the Confirm Order process cannot be completed, the system shall:

1. Reject the confirmation request.
2. Display an appropriate validation or system error message.
3. Inform the customer if the checkout session has expired.
4. Inform the customer if restaurant availability has changed.
5. Inform the customer if menu item availability has changed.
6. Inform the customer if pricing has changed.
7. Inform the customer if fraud detection validation fails.
8. Prevent initiation of the Place Order workflow.
9. Preserve the checkout session whenever possible.
10. Record failure details in application logs.
11. Maintain transactional consistency.
12. Allow the customer to retry after resolving the identified issue.

---

#### 3.5.17.13 Acceptance Criteria

The Confirm Order functionality shall be considered successfully implemented when all the following conditions are satisfied:

1. Only authenticated customers can confirm orders.
2. Checkout session ownership is successfully verified.
3. Checkout session validity is verified.
4. Mandatory legal consent is verified.
5. Delivery address is successfully validated.
6. Delivery type is successfully validated.
7. Payment method is successfully validated.

10. Menu item availability is verified.

13. Confirmation timestamp is generated.
14. Confirmation is associated with the authenticated customer.
15. Confirmation is associated with the active checkout session.
16. Duplicate confirmation requests are handled safely.
17. Successful confirmation events are recorded in audit logs.
18. Checkout status is updated to **Order Confirmed**.
19. Place Order workflow is initiated only after successful confirmation.
20. Complete transactional consistency is maintained.

---

#### 3.5.17.14 Security Considerations

1. Only authenticated customers shall be permitted to confirm orders.
2. Customer authorization shall be verified before processing confirmation requests.
3. Checkout Session IDs shall be validated before processing.
4. All communication shall use HTTPS with TLS encryption.
5. Confirmation requests shall be protected against replay attacks.
6. Sensitive checkout information shall be encrypted during transmission and storage.
7. Personally Identifiable Information (PII) shall be protected according to organizational privacy policies.
8. Role-based access control shall restrict confirmation operations to authorized users and backend services.
9. All requests shall be protected against SQL Injection, Cross-Site Scripting (XSS), Cross-Site Request Forgery (CSRF), parameter tampering, and other injection attacks.
10. Unauthorized confirmation attempts shall be blocked and recorded for security auditing.

---

#### 3.5.17.15 Audit Log Requirements

The system shall record the following audit events during the Confirm Order process:

1. Confirmation timestamp.
2. Customer ID.
3. Checkout Session ID.
4. Confirmation status.
5. Final payable amount.
6. Selected payment method.
7. Selected delivery address.
8. Delivery type.
9. Customer IP Address.
10. Device Type.
11. Browser or Application Version.
12. Checkout Version.
13. Confirmation record identifier.
14. Validation failure reason, if applicable.
15. Audit logs shall be immutable and retained according to the organization's retention policy.

---

#### 3.5.17.16 Assumptions

The Confirm Order functionality is implemented based on the following assumptions:

1. Customer authentication has already been completed.
2. Checkout validation has successfully completed.
3. Mandatory legal consent has been recorded.
4. Final pricing has been calculated.
5. Delivery address has been selected.
6. Payment method has been selected.
7. Order Management Service is operational.
8. Session Management Service is operational.
9. Audit Logging Service is operational.
10. All dependent backend services are operational.
